# Kotodama — development history (archive)

> Verbatim copy of APP_VISION.md as of 2026-10-26, before it was slimmed down. Every session log, measurement and decision made up to then is here. **Don't read this file whole**: grep it for a feature, file or date when you need to know why something is the way it is. New work is recorded in APP_VISION.md, not here.

# Kotodama 言霊 (JLPT Progress Tracker) — Vision, Design & Developer Handbook

> **Read this first after a context reset.** It records *why* the add-on is the way it is, every major design decision (and the alternatives that were rejected), how the matching and data pipelines work, the current state, open questions, and how to work on the code safely. Last updated 2026-10-26 (start at §0's "Session end 2026-10-26 (context clear)" entry): owner tested — ALL CLEAR" entry, then "(last): N1 precision round", "(later still): N2 precision round" and "(later): grammar recall round 13/14" entries, then the "2026-10-26: unattended maintenance pass" one). The add-on is version 3.0.0 (`__init__.py`, `README.md`, `manifest.json`), author BenAstromo, licence AGPL-3.0-or-later (LICENSE).

**2026-10-26: windows open whole on screen (owner: Settings and the main window always opened with the bottom under the taskbar). Not yet seen in Anki.** New `theme.fit_on_screen(window)`: right after the first show (QTimer 0, when Windows' frame / title bar size is known) shrinks the window to the screen's free area (availableGeometry, frame included, 8 px margin) and moves it in; a window wider than the screen keeps its left edge on screen. Used by `ProgressDialog.showEvent` (its height cap ignored the title bar and nothing set its position) and `ConfigDialog.showEvent`. Settings: the Deck & Fields and Kanji & Grammar tabs now scroll (`config_dialog._scrolled`, like Vocabulary Lists), so the dialog never has to be taller than the screen and OK / Cancel stay visible; it opens as tall as its content, then fits. Tests: scratch `qt_fit.py` (800 px offscreen screen: Settings top-to-bottom on screen, OK visible, tabs scroll; a too-tall window shrunk and moved in; a fitting window untouched) ALL PASS; qt_neon ALL PASS; check_gold_text OK, py3.9 OK.

**2026-10-26: header / page seam (owner: the top bar and the scrolling page felt split; picked fade mock-up A). Not yet seen in Anki.** (1) `_PageFade` (progress_dialog): a `PAGE_FADE` = 24 px band at the top of every page scroller's viewport, the window's background (its gradient colour at that height; gold mode's in gold mode) → clear, so the page melts into the dark under the tabs instead of being cut; added by `_NightSky._follow_pages` (so lazily built tabs and grid windows get one), stays put while the page scrolls. (2) Main tabs `QTabWidget#kmaintabs`: theme.py drops the pane's top border (the line under the tabs). (3) Rain: `RainGlass._follow_scroll` moves every drop on the glass, header included (was: only the page's band). Mock-ups: scratch `fade/mock_fade.py` (now / 24 / 48 / 80 px). Tests: `fade/real_fade.py` on the real window — fade on the page and on the Word Grid tab, no top line, header drops move 60 px with a 60 px scroll or leave the glass: ALL PASS; qt_neon ALL PASS; check_gold_text OK, py3.9 OK.

**2026-10-26 (last): N1 precision round — DONE (owner asked). Not yet seen in Anki.** Tatoeba has no unreviewed N1 hits left for make_sample.py (it skips the first 16 per point + every sampled sentence; sample 8's 37 were the last). The Gemini review only showed the first **8** per point (grammar_review.py PER_POINT, md5 order), so `sample11` (seed 20261074, scratch `make_n1.py`) took 70 random N1 hits ranked 9–16 per point (never reviewed) + all 4 N1 hits in the owner's sentences (parse_cache.json): **N1 72/74 = 97% (91–99%)** (owner's 3/4). Errors fixed: 長くなり…短くなり / 二つになり三つになり as 〜なり〜なり (`_nari_nari`: not after に or an adjective's く — the verb なる; corpus: 3 lost, all false) and 成るべく as 〜べく (`beku` regex: (?<!成る), owner's sentence). **Then fixed (owner):** 煮るなり焼くなり / 聞くなり / 行くなって credited ku-naru-ni-naru (the text rule くな(る|り…) saw a verb's く + the particle なり / prohibition な). New `_ku_naru`: not when an uninflected verb token ends at that く and either has kanji or is followed by なり (not なります); keeps 見えなくなる (なくなる is one word), 行きたくなる, よくなる, and kana adjectives the parser mis-splits (ねむく = ね|むく, あったかく, めんどくさく). First tries were too broad (−107, then −11 incl. real kana adjectives); final: −6 of 7,980 corpus hits, all false (3× 煮るなり焼くなり, たどりつくなり, 往くなり, 行くなって), owner's sentences unchanged, baseline −1 (聞くなり…調べるなり). **Also fixed (owner):** 気にしてはいられない credited 〜にしては (`_NOT_BEFORE` "にしては" + いられ|おれ|居られ, like いけ / なら); corpus −1 of 29 (that sentence), owner's sentences and baseline unchanged, rulings 0 failures. Rulings **4,859, 0 failures**, baseline updated, vocab_eval 0 diffs, py3.9 OK. **Next N1 measurement needs a new source** (Tatoeba ranks 9–16 now mostly used: 154 were eligible, 70 sampled).

**2026-10-26 (later still): N2 precision round — DONE (owner asked, medium effort). Not yet seen in Anki.** Fixed the sample-9 N2 errors with guards (`grammar_data.py`): につきたい (就く: `_NOT_BEFORE` "につき" + た[いくかけ]), 実施により得た (`_uru_enai`: not after により), ふるまい (`mai` regex: not after ふる / 振る), 根こそぎ抜け (`_NUKU_LEXICAL` + こそぎ), 目を通して (`_wo_tooshite`: 目), 日にち次第よ / こういう次第だ (new `_shidai`: not before だ / で / よ / です / sentence end — that's "depends on"); left: もの = "a thing" (１キロにもなるものがある, needs meaning). Corpus: −21, all false (also そこにつきたかったら = 着く). **Fresh `sample10` (seed 20261073, B only): N2 49/50 = 98% (90–100%)** (sample 9: 86%, sample 8: 96%); N3 47/50 = 94% (84–98%). Its one N2 error, まことに (誠に) as ことに, guarded in `_koto_ni_wa` (corpus: only the 2 まことに sentences lost). N3 errors left (meaning): 近いものだった (a thing), せいにしても, 暗黙のうちに. N1 not re-measured (sample 8: 37/37; sample 9 drew no N1 set). Rulings **4,785, 0 failures** (samples 9 + 10 added), baseline updated, vocab_eval 0 diffs, py3.9 OK. Stop here: the remaining errors need meaning.

**2026-10-26 (later): grammar recall round 13/14 — DONE, stopped by the agreed rule. Not yet seen in Anki (only grammar / Text Check counts change).** **Fresh measurements (quote these):** recall `recall_sample13` (seed 20261070, annotated blind, slips adjudicated with earlier conventions) **340/383 = 89% (85–92%)** before fixes (round 3 fresh: 90.5%, unchanged); recall `recall_sample14` (seed 20261071, drawn and annotated after the sample-13 fixes) **374/413 = 91% (87–93%) — and the old code scores the same 374/413 on it: the sample-13 fixes moved nothing on fresh text** (stopping signal); precision `sample9` (seed 20261072, all 300 judged) **A 198/200 = 99% (96–100%), N3 47/50 = 94%, N2 43/50 = 86% (74–93%)** — none of the 12 errors come from this round's rules; the N2 ones are old verb-vs-ending confusions (レジ業務につきたい = 就く, 得た成果, ふるまい, 根こそぎ抜け, 目を通して, 日にち次第 = shidai-da, もの = a thing), plus 時として (sample-6 residual), いたって, によるものです, することがあります (a thing), ジョンとテニス (with). **Fixes (`grammar_data.py` only, parser untouched):** から before ずっと (the ず guard was for からず) and 前から; これら/それら/あれら; この方 / そのころ / この世 (one-word この・その・あの・どの + 方/ころ/頃/辺/時/人/日/年/世; not その上 / そのうち); のかい？; Aと Bって / AとBとが (+ が subject); な + ところが (parser's conjunction); のに気付く/驚く = の + に; という after a one-kanji word (洞察力という; not といって / といわれ / というから / というじゃ); お願いいたします = お〜いたす (お願いします stays out); 来れば; てはいない / てもいない = ている (not ても; not てはいられない); っぱなしだった → た; adjective さ/み nouns are nouns (`_NOUN_PATHS`: 優しさが, 若さの; adj-i only, verb stems like 飲み are not); もう〜ましたか (token rule, the text rule skips たか); 片時も / 一瞬も; explanatory のよ / のね; godan volitional + よ/ね (行こうよ). New forms: 〜たことない, 〜た通り / の通りだ / です / である (`_toori`: not the street 混雑した通りで), 〜きれない in kana, 〜難い (`_gatai`: only verbs that take がたい — 信じ・耐え・言い・許し・説明し…; 答え難い / 読み難い are often にくい), 〜てやる (`_te_ageru`: only after a verb's て-form; not やってくる, やっと, やりくり, してやられ, くてやる気, てやまない, nor after 頑張って / 思い切って / 従って / 急いで … where やる is "do"). Also guarded: 大したことない / 大したことはない (not ta-koto / koto-wa-nai). **Corpus (every change checked with dump_ids + diff_sample, 40 random per point judged):** +3,017 / −121 (losses all false credits, 84 of them old といっしょ / といえる "named" errors); judged precision of the new credits ≈ 97–100% per rule after the guards (てやる was 75% before them). Rulings **4,394, 0 failures** (samples 13 + 14 added), baseline updated, vocab_eval 0 diffs, py3.9 OK. **Stopping here:** fresh recall doesn't move; what's left needs meaning (に indirect object vs destination, cause で, ため without に, 弾ける = はじける) or is one-offs. Possible next (owner's call): an N2 precision round on the verb-vs-ending errors above. Noticed, left alone: 気にしてはいられない also credits にしては (old). Dev: patches in this session's scratchpad (patch13a–h.py).

**2026-10-26: unattended maintenance pass (owner asked for bug finding / safety / speed, no new features). START HERE, then the 2026-10-25 entry below for everything still unconfirmed in Anki.** Full details, every check's numbers and the test scripts: `tools/dev/maintenance_log.md`. Backup of the whole add-on from before the pass: scratchpad `backup_jlpt_progress` (path in the log). **Changed (all verified offscreen, none seen in Anki yet):**
- **Data safety:** `overrides.py` saves atomically (temp + replace; a crash mid-save used to leave a cut-off file that loaded as empty, and the next mark wiped every mark); new `overrides.keep_unreadable(path)` keeps `<name>.unreadable-<time>` before an unreadable overrides / kanji / grammar overrides / history / goal_days / nakama file gets replaced. **Overrides is copy-on-write** (marking a tile while Refresh counted in the background could fail the Refresh: "Set changed size during iteration").
- **Deck names** with `_ * \ "`: `card_state.deck_query` escapes them (Anki treats `_`/`*` as wildcards; `"` broke every count); used by `deck_search` and kanji_view's Browser searches.
- **Text Check → Open file:** `text_check.decode_text` (Shift-JIS subtitles of even byte length were decoded as UTF-16 garbage).
- **Debug "Count all notes"** no longer writes `amaterasu_reached` (it permanently unlocked the Sun Diamond, synced, on the Nakama card).
- **Rank "?" window, Japanese:** said only graduated cards count; now matches the 2026-10-21 rule (studied cards count).
- **Qt5 builds (owner: "as compatible as possible"):** tested with a PyQt5 venv (`%TEMP%\qt5v`, `KT_QT=5` in tools/dev/maint/stubenv.py): qt_neon 65/65 and the scenario pass. `WordCell` middle-click tooltip falls back to `globalPos()`; sounds use `neon_sound.sound_effect_classes()` (PyQt6, else PyQt5). Only the star ambience and the animated Amaterasu stay Qt6-only (silent / still). README says so.
- **Tatoeba corpus restored** at `%TEMP%\data\tatoeba\jpn_sentences.tsv`; build_package.py MANUAL list trimmed, release_drafts.md skipped.
- **Speed:** `freq_data.load()` 2.09 → 0.28 s per Anki session (`_split_shared_spellings` indexed by reading; `_kata_to_hira` via `str.translate` in freq_data / meanings / `__init__`); identical output (equivalence test).

**2026-10-25: rain follows scrolling + rain robustness (owner: rain stays put while scrolling; sometimes no rain after coming back from the background; sometimes drops catch no light until switching tabs). Not yet seen in Anki.** (1) `RainGlass._follow_scroll`: every scroll area's vertical bar (`_self_check` connects new ones once a second, e.g. lazily built tabs) moves the drops / running drops (+ trails) / glares / sparks over that viewport by the scroll; drops carried out of it are dropped and new ones form; lights recomputed next frame. (2) `_resume` (back from the background) clears the lights + signature, re-places the glass and repaints it whole (it only ever repainted changed patches). (3) `_self_check` once a second: glass shown and in place. (4) Probable root cause of the lights / rain loss: `_neon_stop` (any main window's close) cleared the shared `_NEON` state (on, items, lights) even with another Kotodama window open; now `_neon_stop(closing)` only drops the closing window's elements when another is open. Not reproduced in Anki, inferred. **2026-10-25: mini widget flicker missing at Anki's start (owner: "widget animations not playing when i first launch anki"). Fix not yet seen in Anki.** Cause (from the code, not reproduced: the offscreen tests have no web view): the 2026-10-24 count-ahead (`prepare_dialog`) started the moment the widget's recount finished, i.e. exactly as the deck list redrew with the flicker; its seconds of pure-Python work (coldest at startup) fought the main thread and the widget sound's render thread (`neon_sound.MINI_LATE` 0.15 s, else silent) for the GIL. **2026-10-22 (after the session end above): mini widget "Today" = the window's after a goal switch; "+N today" on every gain line.** Owner: N2 reached, switched to N1, restarted: window "Today: 20 / 39", mini widget "0 / 39". Cause: a level change resets goal_days.json's day start to the counts at that moment; the window corrects it from the series (`_today_from_series`), the mini widget can't. Fix: `goal.status` passes the series-derived starts into `_day_start(known=…)`, which saves them, so the mini widget agrees once the window has been opened that day (before that it still shows the reset start: it has no history). Not in the share image / Nakama (synced snapshots seen later by others; a "today" there would be stale). **2026-10-22, newest: rain on the glass, N5–N1 sign plates, sparks, rare stutter (owner: rainy "atmospheric faint reflections … the light of the lit up tube can … catch the rain"; "occasionally sparks … the same color as the tube"; "very rarely … a random tube flicker with a very very quiet sound"; picked sign mock-up A; "atmosphere consistent everywhere … optimized … low fps doesn't look nice … we can always pull it back").** **`rain.py` RainGlass:** its own frameless, click-through (`WindowTransparentForInput`), non-focusable, translucent Tool window laid exactly over the window's inside, owned by it — Windows composites it, so a moving drop repaints only the glass. As a child widget over the window it cost ~30 % of a core (every drop repainted the whole stack beneath: dialog, tab pane, cards, bars, tiles); as its own window ≈ 6 % (Vocabulary) / 9 % (Word Grid) of one core in the offscreen test (with rain 7.0 / 10.5 % vs 0.8 / 1.2 % without). 60 fps; only small dirty patches; drop fades at 10 fps, faint trails at 5 fps; drop light cached (`_relight`), light re-read every 8 frames (`progress_dialog.neon_lights(win)`: each neon widget keeps `_lit` = (rect, colour[, strength]) from its last paint; grid zones strength 0.45; dimmed by its flicker). **2026-10-22: goal "Today" = what its link lists (owner: "Today: 2 kanji" with one new note, 蒸気; 気 already known from 30 cards).** Cause: change (5) above made kanji count learning cards, but DAYS_VERSION wasn't bumped, so goal_days.json's day start (899) was the old rule's and an earlier-started learning-card kanji showed up as today's (901 = 899 + 蒸 + it; still the same Anki day at 01:15, rollover 4 am). Fix: `goal._today_from_series` counts the items in `results["history_series"]["gained"]` on today's index (today_parts' keys and filters, marks excluded) and `status` derives the day start from it (`start = known from cards − today`), so the number and the link always agree and per-day stays the day-start figure. goal_days.json still used when there's no series built today (the mini widget, a stale series). Lesson: any change to what counts as known needs DAYS_VERSION bumped, or this path. Test: qt_goal.py "series: …" checks; FAILS none.

**2026-10-22: the neon intro has a sound (owner: "make some potential sound effects for the neon lights flickering on", then picked drafts 1, 3 and 7 of seven).** `neon_sound.py` synthesises it (stdlib, 44.1 kHz WAV in user_files/, one file per window since Qt Multimedia caches by name; older ones deleted) from the flicker patterns of the bars visible when the window opens: main layer = soft hum (1) or electric crackle (3), picked at random per opening, following up to 6 steady bars; each "struggling tube" bar (`is_struggling`: ≥ 3 levels in 0.3 / 0.45 / 0.6) adds draft 7's transformer buzz + ticks (max 2). Fades ~0.5 s after the last bar lights. Quiet like the chime (peak 0.6 baked in, QSoundEffect volume 0.5, winsound fallback); no setting (fewer-settings). Rendering takes ~0.15–0.2 s, so `ProgressDialog.showEvent` runs it on a worker thread and holds the intro at t = 0 (bars dark) until it's ready; after `SOUND_WAIT` 0.6 s it goes on silent rather than out of step. **Mini widget too (owner, same day: "just make it a lot quieter"):** `MINI_VOLUME` 0.1 (main `_VOLUME` 0.5, then 0.42 — owner: "very slightly quieter", ≈ −1.5 dB); silent with the winsound fallback (no volume control there). Its CSS flicker is drawn 2–4 times on arrival, so `mini_widget._neon` queues each draw's patterns (`neon_sound.queue`: one worker, newest draw first) and adds a small script: the first `animationstart` of a `jlpt-neon-*` animation (after NEON_DELAY, only on the page that actually shows) sends `pycmd("jlpt_neon_sound:<run>")`; `play_queued` plays that draw's file, waiting ≤ `MINI_LATE` 0.15 s for it, else silent. Held draws (old numbers) get no sound. `prepare` now only deletes neon files older than 30 s (pending mini files survive). Test: `tools/dev/mini_neon_sound.py` (offscreen; FAILS none). The script's logic was checked in a browser with synthetic events (the pane was hidden, so no real animation frames). Test: `tools/dev/qt_neon.py` ALL PASS with a "neon sound prepared" check (run from a scratch copy; the Tatoeba corpus in %TEMP%\data is gone, a stand-in TSV of parse_cache texts works). Agreed stopping rule: stop tuning when the misses need meaning, fixes need guards against their own false credits, corpus losses ≈ gains, or fresh samples don't move (memory: stop-when-diminishing). No "meaning engine" in the add-on (owner agreed).

**2026-10-21: goal "Today" stuck at 359 after Mark All + unmark (owner).** `goal.counts` step 2 capped known common words at the needed count and took marks first, so with many marks the from-cards part fell; `_day_start` lowered the day's start to it and unmarking then showed +359. Now cards fill the cap first (`xf = x - min(cards, x)`): marks never lower the from-cards count. Also hits the loanword button, not just debug. Mini widget's "Today" line (web view) is not clickable. Gold translation unchanged (checked). Test: `tools/dev/test_gain.py` (offscreen). Not seen in Anki.

**2026-10-21: the rank counts learning cards (owner: "they should match").** The goal's "Today: 3 / 12 words" showed +3 while the rank said "+0 this week": words (JLPT / frequency bars, goal) count once a card is studied (`-is:new`), the rank only once it graduated. Owner chose the rank to follow the words (a daily goal has to credit today's study). `card_state.rank_count` now `-is:new`; `history.rebuild` counts LEARNING and up (plus answers logged with ivl 0); snapshots `rank_v` 4 (older recorded rank ignored, the rebuilt one used); `rank_toast` checks only answers to new cards (type 0); rank text + "?" help + README updated (JA in divine_text). **2026-10-21: チェックする / コピーする count as English loanwords (owner's screenshot).** The JLPT list's FLAGS only cover its own entries, and チェック alone isn't one (only チェックする), so the test skipped it. `jlpt_data.suru_stem` + `is_english_loanword`: a katakana word + する is judged by its katakana part (list flags, else the frequency list's). Only two list entries have this shape (チェックする N4, コピーする N5). Same word as the stem (one meaning = one word): `card_state._found_rank` gives チェックする チェック's frequency row (#1,095), so marking both counts one word. Fresh-install default now marks 2,597. Test: `tools/dev/loan_suru.py`. Evals unchanged.

**2026-10-21: loanword marks vs the schedule, audited (owner asked about conflicts).** No double counting: the schedule never marks anything, it only decides when a marked loanword counts toward the rank; a loanword marked by hand (middle-click) is the same one word in the same queue as one marked by the button (`found_parts` classifies by the loanword test, however it was marked; one key per word); a loanword with a card counts once, as a card (marks only cover words with no card). Marking / unmarking one shifts the queue by one place, so the rank moves by about one. **One real error fixed:** the Near Native ratio line compared "common words you know" (marks count at once) with the rank (loanwords on the schedule): with every loanword marked at the owner's 3,160 cards it would say 100% / 0 outside; now `card_state.loans_not_yet_counted` (marked top-20,000 loanwords the rank doesn't count yet) is taken off the common count first → 84%, 530 outside (tooltip says how many are left out). Test: `tools/dev/loantest.py`.

**2026-10-21: tab-underline crash fixed (owner's log: "wrapped C/C++ object of type _TabUnderline has been deleted").** `_TabUnderline` connected the tab bar's `currentChanged` to a lambda; the widget is deleted when the intro ends, but the lambda connection stayed, so every later tab switch called the deleted widget. Now a bound method (`_tab_changed`, auto-disconnected on deletion, with a RuntimeError guard). `tools/dev/qt_tabline.py` checks tab switches after deletion: no errors. Not seen in Anki.

**2026-10-21: marked English loanwords join the RANK gradually (owner's design). Not seen in Anki.** Problem (owner): marking the loanwords early inflates the rank by ≈2,500 and skips the early ranks; not marking them makes 20,000 need thousands of cards for words you understand; and ranks on the leaderboard / share image aren't comparable. Rule: the k-th marked loanword (sorted by frequency rank r; JLPT-only ones use their level's size 800/1,500/3,900/6,000/13,800) counts toward the rank once learned cards ≥ r − k (owner: "8,000 minus the loanwords already unlocked"); with all 2,536 top-20,000 loanwords marked the last joins at 17,464 cards = rank 20,000, the same point as marking all at once (owner's deck now: 3,160 cards → 3,411 with all marked; `tools/dev/loansched.py`). **Stays a toggle** (owner: not everyone agrees loanwords are learned on sight, not everyone speaks English): only marked loanwords count; the JLPT / frequency bars and the goal count marks at once. Code: `card_state.found_parts` (hand / loan ranks, stored in found_count.json as a dict; an old plain int is read as hand until the next analysis), `loans_counted`, `rank_split`, `cards_for_rank` (inverse, for the debug ±250); `rank_count` applies it (so toasts, seal, mini widget, history snapshots); `history.rebuild` replays it per day (snapshot `rank_v` 3: older recorded ranks are replaced by the rebuilt ones). Rank split line "3,411 counted = 3,160 learned cards + 251 English loanwords + 0 marked found" + tooltip explaining the schedule (JA pattern). Nakama snapshot: `found_loan` = loanwords counted, new `loan_marked`; card line "251 English loanwords counted (of 2,536 marked)"; share image: "incl. 251 English loanwords" under the count. Tests: `tools/dev/loantest.py` (schedule, inverse, 17,464 → 20,000), `tools/dev/qt_loanshare.py`. This settles most of to-do (4b); still open there: the History chart's recorded step for the JLPT / frequency lines (gains already ignore it) and the Near Native ratio line counting marks.

**2026-10-20 (late night): rank "?" window — the "lists fall short" paragraph narrowed to N2 / N1 (owner).** Was: the lists "do not reflect the true scale of the JLPT … If you only study the words on these lists, you will fall short of true proficiency". Now (EN + JA, `upto_n2` computed): up to N3 the lists cover most of the vocabulary (lower levels are written for learners; the owner rejected "the words you pick up along the way fill the rest": vague, and users like the owner want to track every word they learn); from N2 the lists are **still the words most relevant to the test but not enough on their own**; from N2 the tests use real written Japanese and list-only study falls short (≈4,853 up to N2 vs ≈6,000; 8,066 up to N1 vs 10,000–13,400); for N2 / N1 study the lists first, then the most common words (the goal's two steps); vocabulary is only part of passing at every level. Told the owner the honest version: by count the lists cover ≈81–88% of the common estimates at every level up to N2 (N3 and N2 alike); the difference at N2 / N1 is the kind of text (authentic written Japanese), and N1's gap is the big one; no list promises a comfortable pass. The milestone lines ("800 Words (True N5 Level)" …) are unchanged. Checked: `tools/dev/qt_rankinfo.py` renders both modes.

**2026-10-20 (late night, later): JLPT goal N2/N1 = two steps + frequency bars named by size (owner). Not seen in Anki.** (1) **N2 / N1 word goals** (`goal.counts`, `_list_ranks`): the modern size (6,000 / 13,400) in two steps: **step 1 = the JLPT word lists up to that level** (most likely on the test, first), **step 2 = common words not on those lists** (0-based ranks < N whose rank isn't a list word's, not suspended; known by card or mark), counted only up to N − list size (`need`), so common words never replace list words; status gives `steps` {lists: (k, n), common: (k, need)}. Card: "Step 1 · JLPT word lists up to N2: 3,100 / 4,853 (the JLPT Vocabulary bars and grids)", "Step 2 · then common words not on the lists: 0 / 1,147 (the Frequency grid, most common first)" (green when done) + "Why two steps: …"; dialog shows both steps' remainders; History line "Goal words (N2)" = list levels + step 2, whose past is estimated (today's step 2 scaled by the f6000 / f13400 line). Why (owner): a filled top 6,000 alone fills only 45% of the N2 list bar (38% N1; N5 81%), because many list words are rare in writing (median N2 word #5,866) and ~450 have no rank; the lists hold the words most likely on the test, the size estimate says how many are needed. (2) **Frequency bars named by size** (`freq_data.TIER_NAMES` = "Top 800" … "Top 13,400", "Near Native"; `TIER_LEVEL` keeps the level each size follows; `tier_description` = "The 3,900 most common words of written Japanese (about the vocabulary size N3 asks for, a modern estimate; not the test's own words)"): bar labels "Top / 800", grid titles "Most Common Words — Top 3,900", History legend "Top 3,900", word-hover "JLPT N5, but past the top 800 words: …"; Frequency description rewritten (sizes follow modern estimates, words are written Japanese's not the test's, use the JLPT bars for the test's words, written Japanese matters more at N2 / N1 — reading sections are newspaper-style articles and essays; each bar includes the ones before it). Evidence: written frequency matches the lists better the higher the level (33% of list words up to N5 in the top 800, 70% up to N3 in the top 3,900, 80% up to N1 in the top 13,400). README updated (Size bars, new JLPT Goal section). Rank "?" text: "the modern N1 vocabulary estimate". Japanese for all. Tests: `tools/dev/qt_goal.py` (steps, cap, translations), `tools/dev/qt_overview.py` (renders the whole Overview from fake results; run from a folder with qt_goal.py), `tools/dev/overlap.py` (list ↔ frequency overlap). Checks: grammar_eval 4,199 / 0 / 0, vocab_eval 0, gold text OK, py3.9 OK, package 56 files.

**2026-10-20 (late night): intro timer crash fixed (owner's error log).** `ProgressDialog.showEvent`'s neon-intro `tick` raised `TypeError: NoneType += float` every 16 ms: `_INTRO` is module-global, and `done()` (closing mid-intro) sets `t` to None (another window's intro also replaces it) while the old timer kept ticking. Each timer now remembers its `run` and stops when `_INTRO["t"]` is None or the run changed. Not seen in Anki.

**2026-10-20 (late night): JLPT goal BUILT (owner's idea). Not seen in Anki.** Pick the JLPT level you're taking and the test date; Kotodama works out what to learn per day, adaptively. New `goal.py` (logic) + `goal_view.py` (Overview card + dialog). **Rules (owner):** the test covers every level up to the goal (N3 = N5+N4+N3), vocab / kanji / grammar with the bars' "known"; **words (owner, same night, after research): N5–N3 goals count the JLPT word lists (the JLPT Vocabulary bars), N2 / N1 goals count the frequency list's top 6,000 / 13,400 (the Frequency bars; `goal.FREQ_GOALS`, `vocab_source`)** — the JLPT has published no lists since 2010, common modern estimates are 800 / 1,500 / 3,750 / 6,000 / 10,000 (ours: 3,900, and the owner's 13,400 for N1 kept); measured overlap (scratchpad overlap.py): only 222 of 682 N5 list words are in the top 800 (傘 #4,943, 帽子 #3,371; median N5 word #1,229) and 348 of the top 800 are N3–N1 words, so written frequency fits badly at N5–N4, while the lists stop at ≈ 8,066 words up to N1 vs 13,400 (N2 4,853 vs 6,000), and N2/N1 are what jobs and schools rely on. Kanji and grammar always use the lists. Explained in the dialog (`goal_view.WORDS_EXPLAINED`) and on the card (`_words_note`); History draws `f6000` / `f13400` as "Top 13,400 words" for N2 / N1; **finish line = 14 days before the test** (`BUFFER_DAYS`, fixed); per day = what was left at the start of today ÷ days left to the finish line (today included), recomputed daily, so falling behind raises it; it doesn't shrink during the day ("today" counts against it). Day start = the last **card** counts seen on an earlier day (`user_files/goal_days.json` v2, per deck; a new goal / kind starts at the first count seen today; a lower count — lapse, suspension — lowers it for the day). **Marks are not study (owner, same night):** words / kanji / grammar known only through a mark (★ found, "Mark English loanwords as found", basic particles; no card) still count as known, but `counts()` returns them separately (found) and `status()` takes them off what's left at once (per day drops the moment you mark; un-marking raises it) and never adds them to "Today" (one click used to fill the day). Loanwords in the goal pools: N3 lists 191 of 3,026 (6%), top 6,000: 562 (9%), top 13,400: 1,615 (12%). **Vocab leads:** one-number places show only words; the Overview card shows all three. Grammar only when the grammar results have anything known (no source → no number). Tight = more than 40 words a day (amber, names the next test). Phases: plan / review (last 14 days: "Review time" + what's still left) / done / past ("How did it go?" + "Set your next goal…"). No streaks, nothing lost. Config `jlpt_goal` {level, date, set}. Test dates = first Sunday of July / December (`test_dates`), plus "Other date…" (countries with only December, dates abroad). **Shown:** Overview card "JLPT Goal" under the JLPT Vocabulary card (head, Per day, Today x / y, Still to learn, tight line, the 14-day note; "Set a JLPT goal…" / "Change…"); dialog previews "about N a day until <finish>" before saving, "Remove goal"; mini widget "Today: 9 / 14 words" + a thin bar (green when done, **no emoji**: owner); History tab: "JLPT goal" checkbox (only while a goal runs), the chart runs on to the test day with "Words up to N3" (teal #64ffda, gold #fff0b0) and a dashed grey "On track" line from the day the goal was set to the total at the finish line, markers "Finish line" and "JLPT N3" (chart lines may now hold None); share image "Aiming for JLPT N3 · December 2026" under the frequency bar; share pop-up got **two checkboxes (owner): "Show time with Kotodama" and "Show JLPT goal"** (each only when there's something to show; remembered as `share_show_loyalty` / `share_show_goal`); Nakama snapshot `goal` "N3 2026-12-06" (None when unticked or past; server stores as-is, no redeploy), shown as a line under the rank. History's "no projected dates" rule stands: the goal is the user's own date, not a forecast. Gold mode: all text via divine_text (EXACT + goal PATTERNS, `_date_ja`, `_goal_counts_ja`); `check_gold_text.py` now also checks goal_view.py and flattens nested `a if x else b if y else c`. **Tests:** `tools/dev/qt_goal.py` (offscreen, in-memory config, goal_days in its own folder): dates, per-day maths (49/day for 1,650 words over 34 days), same-day fixed target, next-day adaptation (50), no-grammar-source, phases, 14 Japanese strings, renders of every place (both modes). Checks: grammar_eval 4,199 / 0 failures / 0 diffs, vocab_eval 0 diffs, gold text OK, py3.9 OK, package 56 files.

**2026-10-20: restore + keep-your-code (owner). Built; server DEPLOYED 2026-10-20 (owner's OK; version 8581290f-f2f2-46c0-9397-82fb2a524b6e; live smoke test create → leave hidden → rejoin same code → delete: OK, test profile removed). Add-on side not seen in Anki yet.** (1) **Loyalty in the collection:** loyalty now counts *calendar months* that are over with ≥ 1 recorded day (was 30.4-day periods; October counts from 1 November), merged with collection config `kotodama_loyalty` {months: ["YYYY-MM"…], sun: date} (AnkiWeb-synced; union, written only when a month is added); `sun_reached()` = add-on config flag or the collection's, copied across. Survives reinstall, new PC (after a sync), the AnkiWeb version. (2) **Nakama login in the collection:** `nakama._mirror` (on every `save()`, writes only on change) keeps `kotodama_nakama` = {id, key, active, name, friends}; `adopt_collection()` (first `state()`, after each sync, when the tab opens) takes it over when this install has none (active → joined, else parked; friends merged). The key sits in the user's own synced collection (accepted). (3) **Leave keeps your code:** server `POST /profile/<id>/leave` {key} sets name '' and data "{}" (row kept; `list` hides data "{}"; a PUT from a left profile skips the once-a-minute limit; cron frees it after 400 days); client `leave()` parks {id, key} (falls back to DELETE on a server without /leave — 404 "Not found"), `join()` reuses a parked code with a PUT ("No such profile" / "Wrong key" → a new code); join view shows "Welcome back: you'll keep your code ABCD-EFGH." (divine_text pattern); about text + Leave confirm reworded. (4) **Nakama list:** right-click any member → Copy code (Remove for friends only); two members with the same name show "…PQ2X". Typing only a code to log in was rejected (anyone could claim anyone's code without the key; the synced collection carries the key instead). **Tests:** scratchpad `test_restore.py` against `wrangler dev --local` (port 8799): 21/21 (join, mirror, leave hides + parks, rejoin same code at once, reinstall restore, new PC while left, wrong key → new code, old-server fallback, upgrade mirrors, loyalty month merge across PCs, Amaterasu flag both ways); owner's real nakama.json untouched; row render checked. **To deploy:** in `server/`, `npx wrangler@4 deploy` (schema unchanged). Old add-on versions keep working (their Leave = DELETE).

**2026-10-20 (round 4): short causative-passive (歌わされた / 待たされた). Not yet seen in Anki.** **Parser (`sentence_parser.py`):** new rule a-row + される → godan verb (not す / ぬ verbs: 話させられる; 死なされる vs honorific なされる), path element e.g. `わされる`; in `lookup_full` it's the last resort (`short_cp` → `wide = pot = True`), so any real reading wins, even a kanji verb found from kana (飛ばされた = 飛ばす, 減らされた = 減らす); and in kana text it's dropped when the 〜す form is the reading of a lexicon 〜す verb of its own (`_su_verb_readings()`: まかされた = 任す, すまされない = 済ます, めぐらされる = 巡らす — these kana forms stay the old split, unchanged). `SHORT_CAUSATIVE` (parser, imported by grammar_data) = 〜す verbs that are just a short causative (待たす 飲ます 泣かす 笑わす 驚かす 聞かす 読ます 食わす 黙らす 立たす 言わす 歩かす 働かす 書かす 行かす 買わす 払わす 作らす): exempt from that kana guard. **Grammar:** `_short_causative_passive(t)` (the new path shape, or a SHORT_CAUSATIVE verb + される) → serareru-saserareru; passive is kept as well (owner's ruling: 働かされた must count as passive; it ends in the passive される; the line in `_passive` that would have dropped it was dead code before the parser rule). **Checked:** every Tatoeba sentence with a-row + され (487): 43 re-tokenised, all to the right verb (買わされた 買う, 働かされた 働く, 並ばされた 並ぶ, 気づかされた 気づく, のまされた 飲む, またされた 待つ; vocab and Text Check used to see 歌 + わ + された[する]); grammar +111 serareru (samples all correct), +4 に credits, −1 て (出て行かされた, inside 〜ていく). vocab_eval 0 diffs, rulings 4,199 / 0 failures, baseline updated, gold text OK, py3.9 OK. Left: 曇らされる reads 曇る (曇らす not in the lexicon; harmless), kana あわされた / なやまされた keep the old split.

**2026-10-20 (round 3): fresh measurement + quick fixes. Not yet seen in Anki.** **Fresh samples after rounds 1–2 (quote these):** precision `sample8` (seed 20261060; I judged all 337) **A 198/200 = 99% (96–100%), N3 48/50 = 96%, N2 48/50 = 96%, N1 37/37 = 100%** (round 8 was 98 / 96 / 92 / 96: no regression, all within CIs); recall `recall_sample12` (seed 20261061, annotated blind before looking at the detections, then slips adjudicated) **353/390 = 90.5% (87–93%)**, 3 clear wrong detections in 369 (round 8 fresh: 92%, 88–94% → unchanged within the CI; random sentences are dominated by N5 particles, which rounds 1–2 hardly touched). Fresh misses: と "with" (合う, キス, 話したがる), で means/place before は (フランス語では, 沖縄では), だ before けど, つづける in kana, ため without に, どんなことか. **Quick fixes after the measurement (so sample 8 is no longer fresh):** token rule 上 after a plain / た verb or an adjective (な for na-adjectives) → ue-de (verbs only, + で) / ue-ni (+ に / 、); not after the て-form or a bare stem (通って上に昇った); 机の上で / その上 never reach it. rashii-typical-of: noun / katakana / あなた + らしく (君らしくない, 男らしく振る舞う; not 素晴らしく, 汚/憎/愛/嫌/馬鹿/誇/阿呆/長) and 春/夏/秋/冬らしい. Three sample-8 errors guarded: 依然として (not "as"), ためにとって (取っておく), といってもいい (+ という〜ても not "named"; といっても過言ではない stays, owner ruling). Na-adjective そう now excludes only 多分 / たぶん (an adverb-first POS test wrongly dropped 大変そう). Corpus: +139 / −81 (losses all false credits). Rulings 4,188, 0 failures, baseline updated; vocab_eval 0 diffs, gold text OK, py3.9 OK. **Owner asked whether to add "meaning understanding":** answered no inside the add-on (an LLM needs internet/key/cost and sends sentences out; GiNZA-type parsers are hundreds of MB of compiled deps and still not meaning; both make unfixable fuzzy errors); use a model as a dev-time reviewer instead (as with the Gemini batches). **Stopping rule agreed in spirit:** stop tuning when the misses need meaning, fixes need guards against their own false credits, corpus losses ≈ gains, or fresh samples don't move.

**2026-10-20 (round 2, owner: "find more grammar misses without accidentally giving false credit"). Not yet seen in Anki.** Source: OpenJLPT examples that miss their own point (scratchpad `misses.py`, 181 → fewer). All in grammar_data.py; every change corpus-checked (Tatoeba 249k, old vs new, samples of every point read, then the false gains fixed and re-run). **Bugs fixed:** (a) detector anchor (the quick pre-check fragment) was computed after stripping a leading て/た, so ていない's anchor was いな and polite ていません could never match (まだ来ていません); now computed on the part as `_tail` sees it. (b) `_tail` built particle + ない's polite form like a verb (わけがない → わけがぎません): は/が/も/じゃ + ない now → + ありません (わけがありません, ことはありません, ものではありません, たことはありません, べきではありません…). (c) `_NOT_AFTER` had "と言う" twice (二度と言わない's guard was overwritten): merged. **New:** a closing 」/』 fills the 〜 before と / って cores (「さくら」という店, 「…」と言いました, 「…」とばかりに; `_to_iu_named` accepts it); later gapped parts of 2+ chars may be voiced (食べたり飲んだりした; not a lone た: もう〜た / たら〜た stayed); `_COMMA_GAP_IDS` = tari-tari-suru (読んだり、聞いたりする); EXTRA_FORMS for compound particles' adnominal / written forms (に対する/に対し, に基づく, にわたる/にわたり/に渡り, に沿う, をめぐる/をめぐり/を巡り, を除く, に応え(る), に先立つ, にあたり, に向け, に応じ, に即する, に則る, を中心として/とした/とする, に反する/に反し) with guards (`_NOT_BEFORE`: に向け / に応じ only before 、 or 〜た + noun; にあたり not before ま/な (祖母にあたります); に先立た (predeceased); をめぐら/めぐっ/をめぐるの; をめぐり only before 、; を除い/除か/除て (remove); no kanji 渡る (アメリカに渡った), 巡る (touring), kana こたえる (胃にこたえる), kana のぞく (peek)); polite/casual/kanji forms: ませんように, ものじゃない/もんじゃない, に決まってる, もんか (not もんかな/ね), てもさしつかえありません + 差し支え + でも〜, でしかたがない/でしょうがない/て仕方ない…, でならない, たとおり/のとおり/どおり, sentence-first とはいえ, はずなのに/はずなので, てくださいます (not くださいません: a request), てもかまわない/でも構わない (kana かまわない was missing), だれか/なにか, だれも/なにも〜ない (not こんなにも, なにもの), に来る/に帰る for ni-iku (not 早め/通り/初め…に), より〜の方が (not より多くの方が); token rules: ずつ after any word (二つずつ, 少しずつ), lone 目 after number+counter (二回目), na-adjective + そう (元気そうだった; not when the word is first an adverb: 多分そうだ), 言った/述べた/思った…ように → like; `_mono_de_wa_nai` needs a verb (dict / た) before もの (そんなものじゃない out); しか + しかたが/ない/の/あ isn't しか〜ない (3日しかたたない stays); `_baai_wa`, appearance そう not after 本当に/確かに/まさに/実に. OpenJLPT own-example recall: N5 88→93%, N4 91→94%, N3 86→90%, N2 88→92%, N1 88→89%. Corpus vs round 1: ≈ +2,400 credits, ≈ −250 (read: the losses are false credits — でしょうがない's でしょう, なきゃよかった, 時間通りに来る, 祖母にあたります…). - **(2e) bar intro:** the freeze was `_apply_gold_now` right after the first paint re-setting the identical window style sheet (full re-polish) + the divine_text widget walk; `theme.apply` now skips an unchanged sheet and the walk only runs once gold mode has been on (`_was_gold`). The intro is now progress-based (`_INTRO["t"]`, advanced by at most 0.05 s per tick, first tick after the event loop is idle), so a stall pauses the bars instead of snapping them to the end; `done()` resets it. Real-Qt test with a forced 1 s stall: no jump > one frame.
- **Neon intro (owner 2026-10-19, replaces the grow-in):** bars keep their real length from the first frame; each LevelBar / RankBar / BeltBar starts as an unlit tube (fill at `NEON_OFF_FILL` 0.16 opacity, no glow, text at 0.5) and flickers on with its own random pattern (`_flicker_pattern`: 0.05–0.4 s dark, 2–4 stutters, ~30% of sparks half-lit, then on), seeded per window (`_INTRO["run"]`) + widget; `_intro_light(widget)` → painter opacity per layer. 60 fps timer, `INTRO_SECONDS` 1.3, same stall-safe clock as above. Not in Amaterasu mode. Frames: `neon_intro_frames.png` in the add-on folder (delete after review). Note: `random.Random` rejects tuple seeds on Python 3.11+ (an exception in paintEvent aborts PyQt6).
- **Neon intro in the mini widget too (owner 2026-10-19):** the pattern + unlit levels moved to `theme.py` (`flicker_pattern`, `NEON_OFF_FILL/TEXT`; progress_dialog imports them). `mini_widget._neon(rows_html)` gives each `jlpt-mini-fill` / `jlpt-mini-small-fill` / `jlpt-mini-text` a class `jlpt-neon-fN/tN` with its own `@keyframes` (opacity, `step-end`, ends at 1), so the 5 JLPT bars + rank + belt bars flicker independently. **Final form (owner confirmed it shows, 2026-10-19):** a temporary debug log showed Anki redraws the home screen 2–4 times within a second of arriving, and the first draw (the only neon one) was replaced before it was shown. Now every draw within `NEON_WINDOW` (1.2 s) of the first neon draw also gets the flicker; later refreshes draw it lit. The inline script was removed (it never reported from Anki; pycmd-before-ready likely broke it): CSS only, `animation-delay` `NEON_DELAY` 0.3 s (unlit until the page is painted), and a draw with old numbers while a recount runs uses `NEON_HOLD_DELAY` 4 s (the fresh redraw replaces it). Anki's "Reduce motion" (owner has it on) only sets a `reduce-motion` body class that Anki's own Svelte screens read; it doesn't affect add-on CSS. **More variety (owner: not the same order each time):** `theme.flicker_pattern` start delay 0.05–0.75 s, 1–5 stutters, 20% struggling tubes (fast dim buzz), 25% a last dip after catching, squeezed to ≤ `NEON_MAX_SECONDS` 1.6; main window `INTRO_SECONDS` 1.8. Earlier attempt, superseded: **Revised after the owner's screen recording (it didn't show, and they want it every time):** armed by `gui_hooks.state_will_change` whenever Anki moves *to* the deck browser from another state (startup, review, overview…), used up by the first draw that shows the widget; refreshes while you stay there (sync, recount) draw it lit. Likely cause of "didn't show": the CSS animation started when the page was built, before Anki painted it. Now `_NEON_START` (inline script) pauses the neon animations at frame 0 and plays them 150 ms after `load` (and only when visible; not rAF, throttled in some webviews), with a 4 s fallback; if the script can't run the CSS plays on its own. A draw with old numbers while a recount runs (`fresh` false) holds the bars unlit and stays armed, so the fresh redraw ~1 s later does the flicker. Never in Amaterasu mode. Checked in the browser pane by pausing the animations at 0 / 250 / 450 ms / end (scratchpad `mini_neon.py` writes the preview page).
- **(2f) contexts, as a balance shift (owner: avoid inflation):** `kanji_data.weigh_contexts` (used by kanji and grammar): score = mean × f(n) / deck average f, f(n) = 0.7 + 0.3·(1 − e^(−n/2)); the deck's average score is unchanged, few-card items drop, many-card items rise. 8k synthetic deck: average 0.577 → 0.577; n=1 0.584 → 0.519, n=3 0.564 → 0.571, n≥5 ≈0.57 → 0.61. Plain mean kept in `card_mean`; hover says "from N learned cards" (was "average of"); README updated.

- **Server** (`server/`, excluded from the package via build_package SKIP_DIRS): `worker.js` (Cloudflare Worker), `schema.sql`, `wrangler.toml`. Live at **https://kotodama-nakama.kotodama-anki.workers.dev** on the owner's free Cloudflare account (login benastromo; workers.dev subdomain `kotodama-anki`, renamed by the owner from the auto-made `benastromo-2a5`; D1 database `kotodama-nakama` id d2562d8e-c714-49c5-a754-5d1adc9af8f3, region WNAM). API: `POST /profile {name,data}` → `{id,key}`; `PUT /profile/<id> {key,name,data}`; `DELETE /profile/<id> {key}`; `GET /profiles?ids=A,B` (≤ 60). id = 8-char code from `23456789ABCDEFGHJKMNPQRSTUVWXYZ` (shown ABCD-EFGH), key = 32 random bytes hex, stored as SHA-256. Limits: body ≤ 8 KB, data ≤ 6 KB, name ≤ 32 printable chars, one update per profile per 60 s (the add-on treats that 429 as "next time"), creation 5/min per IP (`[[ratelimits]]` binding), daily cron deletes profiles not updated for 400 days. Free plan = no card on file: over the limits requests fail, never billed. Cloudflare's bot filter 403s Python's default user-agent: the add-on sends `Kotodama-Anki`, keep it. **Redeploy:** in `server/`, `npx wrangler@4 deploy` (wrangler login: the owner ran `npx wrangler@4 login` in their own PowerShell — `&&` doesn't work in PS 5.1; my background logins kept failing with a CSRF mismatch because a stale localhost:8976 callback tab auto-reloaded). Local test: `npx wrangler@4 d1 execute kotodama-nakama --local --file=schema.sql` then `npx wrangler@4 dev --local --port 8799` and set env `KOTODAMA_NAKAMA_URL=http://127.0.0.1:8799`.

- **(A) Speed.** (a) **Analysis off the main thread:** `card_state.run_in_background(parent, work, done, label, quiet)` wraps `aqt.operations.QueryOp` (falls back to running inline without it) under `card_state.ANALYSIS_LOCK` (one analysis at a time; it also serialises history.json writes). The window open (`__init__._open_progress_dialog`: work = collect + analyse + maturity + `ProgressDialog.compute_extras`), Refresh / Take snapshot (`_do_full_refresh` → `_recompute_results` in the background → `_apply_refresh` on the main thread; a click while counting is ignored; snapshot tooltip after it's done) and the mini widget (silent, no progress window) all use it. Progress label "Kotodama: counting your deck…" (Anki's own window: not gold, not checked). `ProgressDialog.compute_extras` = kanji + grammar + found count + `history.record` **then** `build_series` (order matters: today's snapshot is part of the series). (b) **`card_state.deck_notes(deck)`**: one `select id, mid, flds from notes` for the deck (`DeckNote`: `.type_name`, `name in note`, `note[name]`), memoised until count / sum(mod) / the nid set changes; collect_words, sentence_texts, kanji `_field_texts` and grammar fields mode use it (no more `col.get_note`). (c) **`sentence_parser.SentenceWords`** + `user_files/words_cache.json`: sentence text → [[display, reading]], same signature as the parse cache (`grammar_data._parse_sig`), atomic write. (d) **Mini widget recounts only on change:** `_change_key` = settings + `count/sum(type)/sum(suspended)/sum(ivl≥21)/sum(did)/sum(odid)` over cards + `count/sum(mod)` over notes + overrides.json mtime (≈0.01 s); a stale key shows the last numbers (same settings) and starts a background recount, whose end redraws the deck list; "not configured" is remembered too (no recount loop). history's daily deck-list hook uses `mini_widget.cached_results()` and never waits for the lock. **Measured (`tools/speed_test.py`, now two sessions per size; seconds, 30k | 60k):** window open first session ever 29 | 56 (was ≈35 | 66, now in the background), **next Anki session 4.3 | 6.0**; Refresh 1.5 | 2.5; mini widget recount 0.8 | 1.1 (was 2.1 | 4.2); change check 0.01; peak memory +6–9% (544 MB at 30k). Old vs new code on a 6k synthetic deck: byte-identical results (incl. a second run reading the words cache). **Real-Qt test** (scratchpad `qt_bg_test.py`: the whole package incl. `__init__`, a fake QueryOp running op in a thread, 8k synthetic deck): 26/26 pass. **Still on the main thread:** building the window's tabs (1.7 s on 8k notes) and `_apply_refresh` (1.5 s): widget construction, not in (a)–(d); a possible next speed step. Session 2's `analyse` (≈2.8 s) is freq/meanings data loading on first use. tools/harness.py and grammar_review.py stubs gained `db.first` + `col.models.get` (needed by deck_notes).
- **(B) Grammar recall audit (owner: "there are still grammar points I have that are not detected, like つつ").** Copying the collection was blocked (privacy check), so the owner's 2,970 sentence texts came from `user_files/parse_cache.json`; scratchpad `recall_audit.py` lists points whose written form is in a sentence that isn't credited. Fixed (all in grammar_data.py, each corpus-checked old vs new over 249k Tatoeba sentences and samples read): **(1)** polite endings after a one-kanji ichidan stem or に (見ました, 出ません, 寝なさい, 死にました; `_POLITE_STEM_END`; not any kanji: 覚ました; not に before なさい: 何になさいますか); **(2)** a bare `ない` at the end of 全然〜ない / しか〜ない / なかなか〜ない / 何も〜ない / ほど〜ない… now accepts ません (`_tail`); **(3)** the "longer match wins" rule counted a gapped point's gap: every point inside it was dropped (全然勉強していない lost 〜ている, なかなか上手にならない lost 〜になる). Now a hit is dropped only if it **starts inside one of the longer point's written parts** (each part is a regex group, `_part_spans`); **(4)** くらい/ぐらい extent (`_kurai_extent`: after a verb, adjective or plain noun; not numbers incl. ヶ月, time words, これ/どれ/同じ); **(5)** concessive ながら(も) after an i-adjective or a kanji noun / na-adjective (残念ながら, 子供ながら; not 陰ながら, the 涙/昔/生まれ set, or verb stems like 歩き); 狭いながらも / 生まれながらに no longer also credit "while"; 見ながら now credits "while"; **(6)** 〜って hearsay/quote: んだって, 怖いって有名, Noun + だって at a sentence end or before 聞く/言う (not before a comma: いくら便利だって、 is "even if"); **(7)** sentence-first ついでに; **(8)** 〜んでし no longer matches 〜でしまう (読んでしまった ≠ のです). Final corpus check: 2,460 gains over 60 points (the gap fix alone frees ≈200 〜ている, ≈195 〜くない, ≈100 じゃない inside あまり／全然／ほど〜ない) / 148 losses over 3 points (129 false のです in 〜でしまう / ませんでした, 16 false "while" in 少ないながら / 生まれながらに, 3 あまり(に)); samples of every big point read: correct, except のろいながら (呪う read as 鈍い: lost "while") and rhetorical するじゃないか credited じゃない. Owner's deck: +85 / −5 credits vs this morning's cache. **Left (seen, not fixed):** 読んだって分からない isn't credited たって (the んだって hearsay exclusion catches it); 〜という + content noun (という自覚); に限ります (limited to); nested points still hide inside contiguous longer ones by design (テレビを全然見ません: fine now; 全然〜ません spans).

(2) **Vocab cards that are grammar forms:** `grammar_data.word_card_ids(deck, mappings)`: a note whose mapped *word* field (cleaned, 〜 dropped) equals a grammar key (`key_index`) is credited with **every** id of that key (でも → te-mo-de-mo + de-mo-suggestion; ながら → nagara + nagara-mo); owner: "if the user uses a good dictionary, the use cases are defined on that card". Never the dictionary/example fields. Excluded: kanji-only keys (方 中 上 以上 手前 場合 予定…) and `_WORD_CARD_NOT_GRAMMAR` (出す 切る 合う 始める かける 得る やすい にくい 向き…). Merged into sentence mode's note_ids; the tile's "Your cards" shows the word. Synthetic test (scratchpad `wordcard_test.py`) OK; grammar_eval unchanged. (3) **Subtitle spaces:** `sentence_ids` now credits the union of the text with in-sentence spaces joined (`_join_spaces`: a space between two Japanese chars, kept after よねぞぜわさなかのろだ except まだ) and as written; "question か" is dropped where the joined text has 〜か "or". Owner's 362 spaced sentences: 39 gain, 0 lose. Plus 〜か "or" across a space and past an unknown one-kanji prefix (社長か 副社長に), embedded question across a space (幻想か 分かりません), てある's あれ only before ば (これってあれか, あれほど), につき not before あ/合 (付き合う). (4) **"In your deck, not learned yet" grammar tiles:** grammar now scans never-studied notes too (`deck_notes(deck, seen_only=False)` — its own memo slot; `sentence_texts(..., seen_only)`, `word_card_ids(..., seen_only)`, cards via `deck_search(deck, seen_only=False)`). Such a point has `words` but `cards == 0`: not known (bars, rank, history unchanged), but `GrammarCell._in_deck` draws it lighter with a dashed level-colour outline, sorted right after known points; tooltip/status "In your deck, not learned yet"; grammar legend gets ⬚ "In your deck, not learned" (`_heat_legend_html` when unseen == "Not learned"); Japanese in divine_text PHRASES. - **(1) Grammar round 8: precision + recall** (owner: "really good recall and really good precision; a missed or false point confuses the user"). Fresh samples before fixes: precision `sample6` (seed 20261050) A 196/200 = 98%, N3 96%, N2 96%, **N1 86%**; recall `recall_sample10` (20261051) 364/406 = 90%. **Fresh after all fixes (quote these):** precision `sample7` (20261053) **A 197/200 = 98% (96–99%), N3 96%, N2 92%, N1 96%**; recall `recall_sample11` (20261052) **369/403 = 92% (88–94%)**, wrong detections 4/388. Tuned samples rose a lot (recall 7–10: 94–98%) but the fresh gain is within the CI: say so. Every change also checked by running old vs new code over all 249k Tatoeba sentences and reading random gained/lost hits per point (scratchpad `dump_ids.py` + `diff_sample.py`; two passes, second one fixed the over-corrections). 
  - **Precision fixes:** volitional ようと + 言う/思う/決心/なさる (incl. ようとは思わなかった, ようとも考えた) and question words already answered by their own ても or an embedded か no longer make 〜ようが/ようと concessive (most N1 errors); はもちろん needs も/まで/さえ, a comma, or a non-person topic; 足りない after a noun ≠ に足る; 誰もが / 何もかも ≠ 誰も〜ない; ようなら → you-da, not "like"; 時として、; 落としたら; 言いなり / 道なり; どうしたら…ものか (embedded) ≠ ものか; 以上の / 以上に ≠ 以上は; 立てかける; こと思う (そんなこと思ってない) and と思い出す/込む/つく ≠ と思う; ところによれば; というところ; なりにくい; ばほど needs a conditional ば; うたって; ちっとも; interjection あれ、; 〜までに no longer also credits まで; ままで; ２０分ごとに; まで抜ける; ちょっとした; に…近づく/前に (time 前に excluded unless Xの前に).
  - **Recall fixes:** **godan imperatives** (黙れ！, 行けと言った, 急げ、さもないと; quoted 寝ろと言われた) were never credited; casual ておく (止めとき, 消しといて); だが token → ga-but + da; に-location across a comma (公園には、…いる; ここには、…空間がない; not 時間がかかる); が + 数人 + いる; で-place after Xの前/近く/横… and ~25 more place nouns; で-means ~40 more (船便, スカイプ, 大声, 辞書, 弱火…; not この辞書でよろしいですか); とは思う / とも思う; もらえる and に + なれる potentials; どのくらい / どれくらい; 〜てもよい/良い/よろしい + なくてもよい; ん+じゃない → n-desu and …じゃないかな → kana; noun + か at clause start (本当か、/ マジか。, not １日か２日); ビーチまで + movement verb; 持って帰る / 連れて行く destination; ni-indirect-object skips an adjective before its noun (私たちにおいしい牛乳を与えて); いい counts as a predicate (いいから → kara-reason); 欲しがる etc. (below) get がる + their ta/nai/te-iru.
  - **Parser (`sentence_parser.py`, also Text Check):** adjective stem + がる is one verb (欲しがる was 欲+し+がる); `_bad_chain`: 行く's て/た forms are only 行って/行った, and a bare とく stem isn't a word — **いいとき / 悪いとき / 若いとき were 行く+とく / いる+とく**, now い-adjective + とき.
  - **Still missed / left:** 場合 as a plain noun (どの場合にも) is credited baai-wa; お〜できる (o-suru + potential: お借りできますか); stem + すぎ + ちゃった; ga-subject after some compounds; kurai extent; ge after いとし.

Fixed from that reading: 〜て初めて not after のっ (書くのって初めて) nor before の (にとって初めての), adjective past not いけなかった, に + なって/して + くれる isn't an indirect object (仲間になってくれる), あるな isn't a prohibition, godan causative only with kanji (not もたせかける).
- **Changes.** EXTRA_FORMS: て欲しい, て初めて, にも関わらず/拘らず, ではない/じゃなく (negative copula; だけではなく / ばかりではなく added to their own points so they win), ことが出来る. Tokens: **だった → ta** (missed in every recall sample 2–8), だ+ろ → darou, ん+だろう/でしょう and なの+よ → n-desu, adjective かった → ku-nai-katta (the text search skips かった; not 〜ばよかった), plain verb/adjective + が、 → ga-but (not ようが), **godan causative** 驚かせた/付き合わせて → seru-saseru (せる was never searched), できる/出来る after a する-noun + が/は/も when negative → potential (not 準備ができた), が + number + counter + ある/いる → ga-subject + ga-aru, は after a number, の after numbers/％/くらい/それら, な-adjective before こと/もの (こと is a "particle" in pos_text), 信用するな, 考えろよ, よな, 見ていると、 → to, を取って (`_WO_TE_REAL`: 取る/かける/向ける/加える aren't compound particles after を), 頃 as a lone token, １日中, ９月に１６歳, ように見える/思える → like, か + に気づく → embedded question, 事 nominalizer, `_MOVE` + 進学/入学/到着/移る/飛び出す/飛び込む, `_GIVE` + 与える/明け渡す, に…〜てあげる/くれる (`_BENEFACTIVE`).
- **Still missed (left on purpose or for later):** kana potentials (やれる, もてる: kana-only is too often a misread), ichidan られる passive without an agent (建てられた), で place after words not in `_PLACE_END` (面前で, 田舎での, アフリカで), particle-less nominalizers (走るの嫌, 見たの初めて), やいなや in kana, てもよい, すべきか, 欲しそう.

**2026-10-17 (owner watching TV, asked for background work): parser vocab accuracy (to-do (2)) DONE + grammar round 6 (misses) DONE. Nothing UI-visible except Text Check numbers and grammar counts; not yet seen in Anki.**
- **Vocab eval built:** `tools/vocab_eval.py` + `tools/vocab_cases.json` (250 hand-checked Tatoeba sentences, 18–60 chars: seed 20261030 ×150 tuned on, seed 20261031 ×100) score exactly what the Text Check counts — `text_check.piece_items(tokens)` (new; `check_text` uses it). Gold syntax in the tool's docstring (`~` optional, `a|b`, `a+b`, `?run` = should be unrecognised). `--cases <file>` scores another set; `--update` accepts the baseline (`vocab_eval_baseline.json`). Draft sampler: `tools/accuracy/make_vocab_sample.py` (parser words next to MeCab lemmas).
- **Numbers.** Start (set 1): 63% sentences fully right, word recall 96.2%, precision 95.6%, 5.0 wrong "unrecognised" runs /100 words. **Fresh set 2, before its own fixes: 83% fully right, recall 97.8%, precision 97.8%, 1.5 /100** — quote this one. Now (all 250, tuned): 92%, 98.6%, 98.9%, 1.0 /100. MeCab CUT sentences 63,600 → ~45,800, SWALLOW 26,155 → 25,728.
- **Parser fixes (`sentence_parser.py`):** `lookup_full` prefers fewer endings undone (立てて = 立てる not 立つ's potential; 整える, 触れる) and a godan potential reading always loses to a real word (付けた = つける), except adjectives that only list the spelling (行けなかった ≠ いけない) and honorific なさる etc.; stem + すぎる/がたい/にくい/やすい/づらい rules; なくちゃ/なくっちゃ/なきゃ; ましたら; whole する-verbs conjugate (罰せられる, 関して; not 決する/欲する, kanji before する only); function tokens べき すべき っぱなし どころ らしい らしく らしかった; 彼女のこと no longer 彼 + 女の子 (の in the particle-inside rule); context spellings `_CONTEXT_SPELLINGS`: みる only after て/で (してみた), どおり after a word (時間どおり); 万人 after digits = 万 + 人; noun = verb stem before 、 after a particle → verb (離れ、 行き、 感じ、: owner's 行き case); この間違い / その後悔 split before the kanji; ですかね ≠ かねる; adverbs 単に/別に can't take a compound's kanji (簡単に); a lone kanji cut from its okurigana costs +0.7 (開いたそう); ・ and 々 handling; 何 + counter, numerals + counters not lone (`_NUMERALS`, `_COUNTERS`, counter readings ねん/にち…); 方 → ほう in 話さないほうがいい-type spots (`_hoagain`); standalone 他 → ほか; 月 alone → つき; compounds touching kanji only blocked when that kanji makes a word (以上僕ら). **Tie-break:** exact cost ties go to the more common kanji words via a separate DP key (log10 rank; JLPT level stands in when there's no rank: 日本). Folding it into the cost changed old ties, which were really decided by float rounding — keep it separate.
- **Text Check (`text_check.py`):** a single kanji stuck to a counted word is an affix, neither known nor unknown (定食屋, 交通量, 無関心; `_SUFFIX_KANJI` for non-words like 屋); お/ご/御 before a word and ら after one too; では/だが/そこで/それで/だから mid-sentence aren't vocab; なり after a plain verb isn't なる.
- **Wider kana rule (owner said yes, 2026-10-17; replaces the 10-16 "usually-kana only" limit):** a kanji word may match its hiragana reading (だれ, なに, はる, つきあう, ことば, じょうず, まわる, しっている, うたたね…), with guards, all in `sentence_parser`: common words only (JLPT N5–N3 or rank ≤ 5,000); cost `_COST_KANA_SPELLING` (2 kana 1.8, else 1.3) above real words; in `lookup_full` such a reading (`wide`) loses to any other parse (なれなかった = なる's potential, not 慣れる); in `tokens()` it must start after punctuation, a particle, て, く or ら, never with a particle kana after a word (彼氏はいる, 病気になった, にはなれない), never with して, a function token (しかない ≠ 敷く), or た after く (高くたって); a noun read this way must end at a particle/punctuation (紙+つかれた, 動詞+たの, 人+かどの); after を a verb reading wins (切手をはる = 貼る); `_KANA_NEVER` for traps (指摘, 入る, 足る, 理想, 申す). Also: くらい / ぐらい / がてら are function tokens, におい + て is blocked (において), DP costs are rounded so equal costs tie exactly, ば credited for verb なければ. Result: vocab eval 92 → **95% fully right, recall 99.4%, precision 99.3%**; grammar 0 failures (ni-destination guards for において / 〜をもとに; おく in `_COMPOUND_TE`); owner's deck grammar unchanged (200), text coverage 69.1%.
- **Grammar round 6 (owner: "check for grammar points that are not being detected").** `tools/accuracy/point_coverage.py` (per point: searched?, own examples detected, Tatoeba hits → `point_coverage.tsv`) and `point_samples.py` (random Tatoeba hits per id, trigger in 【】, for precision reading). Bugs found: かのようだ never matched its な/に forms (`_tail` for 〜ようだ), 極まりない blocked after kanji (`_NOUN_SUFFIX`), EXTRA_FORMS for に伴い/伴う, を踏まえ, ちゃいけない/ちゃだめ…, を下さい. **Newly searched points** (were never searched): potential (`_potential`: godan e-row + る, ichidan られる only negative or with its own を, noun + できる, kana only for the てもらえる/ていただける/ていける auxiliaries and 言える, not てはいけない), and `_never_searched` / `_NEVER_SEARCHED_RX`: 〜中, ずに, 〜さ, ほど (degree, not 4年ほど), まま, 〜方, がる, し, さえ, こそ, すら, ぶり, っけ, がち, べく, なり, たる, きり, もの/もん, こと (instruction, ないこと。), とか (hearsay), めく, げ, まい, だに, つ〜つ — each read against its Tatoeba hits. Still skipped on purpose: `_SKIP_PATTERNS` ones and meaning-only points (て reason, まで even, くらい extent, とは). **に indirect object** finds its verb later in the clause (`_give_verb`; not passive agents, 借りがある, という), more `_PERSON` / `_GIVE` words; **に location** with ない after が/は/も (島には店がない; not 興味がない, に関する, ともない) and more `_STAY` verbs. Grammar fixes forced by the parser: がたい ≠ たい, compound particles after のに (のに対して), adjective くても → ても, counters/suffixes nounish (八時の).
- **Grammar numbers.** Fresh recall sample 7 (seed 20261025, before fixes): **363/405 = 90% (86–92%)**, precision in those sentences 371/376; round 5 was 88%. After: 93% on sample 7, samples 3–6 now 88–96% (tuned on, not fresh). Textbook own-examples: N4 83→87%, N3 76→84%, N2 85→88%, N1 83→88%. Owner's deck (harness, same config): learned points 186 → 200, none lost (gained buri, chuu-juu, potential, kata, kiri, koso, mai, mama, mono-mon, sa, sae, shi, sura, zu-ni; each hit read in the owner's sentences — 田中さん, 4年ほど, a bare とか fragment were fixed). grammar_eval 0 failures, baselines updated. py3.9 syntax OK, check_gold_text OK.

**2026-10-16 (latest): kana words + つつ (owner asked: "start the kana-verb parser step but just for things usually in kana"; reported a つつ card not detected).** (1) **`kana_usual.txt`** (115 words) + `tools/build_kana_usual.py`: list words written with kanji that Tatoeba usually writes in kana, measured with MeCab (fugashi/unidic-lite) — qualifies with ≥ 5 kana uses, ≥ 50% of uses in hiragana, reading ≥ 3 kana (いっぱい, きれい, うれしい, すばらしい, かまう, だます, たたく, ふもと, ふさぐ, とがめる, うなずく, よみがえる, あした, ゆうべ, おととい …); `EXCLUDE` (hand-checked) drops MeCab mislemmas and grammar collisions: 出でる (〜ないで), 仕様 (どうしよう), 孵る, 澄ます, 浸かる (見つかる), 撓, 諮る, 仕舞い (〜てしまい), 辺り (〜にあたり), 如何 (行かん), 敵う/適う (stem かない broke しかない / ずにはおかない). `sentence_parser._kana_ok()` reads kana_ok.txt + kana_usual.txt (kana spelling may match, costed like a kanji word); kana_usual.txt is in the parse-cache key and ships like kana_ok.txt. あきらめる is NOT in it (Tatoeba writes 諦める in kana < 50%), as the owner wanted only usually-kana words. Run the builder with the pylib path as 2nd argument when %LOCALAPPDATA%\jlpt_devtools isn't visible (this session copied pylib to the scratchpad). (2) **つつ** (owner's card 「…思いつつめっちゃ心臓バクバクで」): つつ is two kana, so the text search never looked for it, and the parser left つ + つ unknown; now つつ is a FUNCTION_TOKEN and つつ after a verb stem credits tsutsu-tsutsu-mo (not before あ: つつある is its own point). (3) **Compound particles before a comma** (について、, にとって、, に向けて、, をめぐって、 …: `_COMPOUND_TE` particle + verb pairs) no longer credit sequential て (30 sentences; する and を+つく stay real て-forms: こんな風にして、, うそをつかれて、). (4) **Owner confirmed** the nara ruling: the なら inside ならない must not count; the のなら in 「もし君が試験に合格したいのなら…」 is real (case stays present). Results: grammar_cases 3,006 (owner's つつ sentence, しかない ×2, つつある, をめぐって、 added), 0 failures; textbook examples unchanged or up; every recall sample same or up (6: 92→93%, 3: 87→88%, 5: 95%), no correct detection lost in precision samples 1–5.

**2026-10-16 (later): grammar round 5 — DONE (owner away; asked for "something to run in the background").** All in `grammar_data.py` + three parser fixes; nothing UI-visible except grammar / Text Check counts. **Measured on fresh, untouched samples:** recall `recall_sample6` (seed 20261023) **341/389 = 88% (84–91%)** before its own fixes (round 4: 85%, 81–88%; this round's start, `recall_sample5` seed 20261022: 82%, 78–85%); precision `sample5` (seed 20261024, drawn after all recall fixes): **A 293/300 = 98% (95–99%)**, B N3 47/50 = 94%, N2 46/50 = 92%, N1 46/50 = 92% (round 4: 97 / 94 / 96 / 96, all inside the CIs). After the sample-5/6 fixes every annotated recall sample went up (1: 70→86%, 2: 76→87%, 3: 77→88%, 4: 85→90% — untouched this round, 5: 95%, 6: 92%; 5 and 6 were tuned on) and no correct detection was lost in precision samples 1–5. grammar_cases.json **3,002 cases, 0 failures** (recall samples 5+6 and precision sample 5 added); baseline 4,528 sentences, every diff group reviewed by sampling.
- **Detector fixes:** `_tail` uses godan endings for る after an あ/う/お-row kana (`_GODAN_RU`): がある matched "があな(たに)", くなる "行くなよ"; くる's tail takes くれ only before ば (見せてくれ was 〜てくる); `_NOT_BEFORE` くなる (行くなら), て下さい, がいる (がいっぱい); `_NOT_AFTER` たらた (もたらした, はたらいた), くなる 亡, 続ける/始める/終わる after て/で (the verb alone), につき (職につき = 就く), において (大体において), なりに/なりの (弓なり, 鈴なり, いきなり), として after っ (mimetic とする); EXTRA_FORMS 〜て下さい, 〜が大好き/大嫌い, 〜たことがない/はない, 〜ねばならない; ni-tsuite not before ゆく; purpose ようと not before a quote verb in its clause (しようと…言った).
- **Token rules:** けど / けれど (too short for the text search); だから after a word = reason (not ものだから); stray ます / くださいませ; ても func token; では mid-sentence = で + は (+ de-place); ここ/そこ/あそこ/どこ; standalone ない / 無い / なかった (not だけでなく); stem + 過ぎる; 〜的 words → teki and 的な → na-adjective; こと nominalizer after a verb (not ことがある / ことにする / 驚いたことに / ということ / ことである); は / が after a nominalizing の; が after こと/もの (not ことができる / がある); は not inside てはいけない / なくてはならない; だ before と; んだ/のだ after な / だった; のなら; ka-question after でしょう / だろう / のです and かよ; かな → kana-kashira; よね → yo; kara-from after any noun (not そば/こと/べからず/からには/からして/から見る/からいう/から成る) and noun + movement verb; kara-reason after のだ/んだ; ni-time not in になる; 次に not destination; ni-location verb skips adjectives (どの群れにも…黒い羊がいる); na-prohibition before よ; も after any noun and after に/で/から/へ (と only after a noun); でもどう; 払う in `_GIVE`; ごらん honorific; 参ったよ not kenjougo; numbers (punct tokens) no longer end a clause (`_is_num`); de-place after ここ/外 (`_PLACE_WORDS`), katakana names, 〜内; sequential て before a noun when a verb follows (`_TE_ADVERBS` excluded: はたして, 従って, 思い切って …); と conditional without a comma unless a quote/knowing verb follows (`_QUOTE_VERBS`) or といい / とおり; ましたら → tara, not ta.
- **Parser (`sentence_parser.py`, also changes Text Check):** `_merge_katakana_names` — a katakana run split with an unknown piece or 1–2-letter pieces is one unknown name (スティーブ was ス+ティー+ブ, メアリー メ+アリ+ー, ベルリン ベル+リン, フランス フラン+ス; テニスコート stays); Noun + 的 is one word, credited to the noun (個性的 was 個 + 性的 "sexual"); set phrases with a particle inside (気をつける, 間に合う) can't start inside a kanji compound (電気をつけない, 谷間にあった). `kinds_fix.txt` + `tools/build_kinds_fix.py`: しかる → v5 (its meanings entry is 然る, so 叱られた was 叱 + ら + れ + た).
- **One ruling changed:** 「もし君が試験に合格したいのなら…」 nara moved absent → present (the old ruling was about the なら inside ならない; this のなら is real). Mention to the owner.
- **Residuals seen (not fixed):** kana verbs and words split (すんでいる, ふもと, くらい, おススメ, あきらめる, とがめる, ふさぐ: kana spellings of kanji words aren't in the lexicon unless kana_ok.txt lists them — a broader rule for ≥4-kana verbs is the next parser step, measure it with the MeCab tool), 内向的 (内向 not a word), に indirect object for 話しかける / 水をやる, reason て vs sequential て (喜んで, もらえて嬉しい), literal ものではない / ことはない, といっても過言ではない, 彼はもちろん, になりにくい, しようとは思わなかった, ボディーソープで as a place, 混同するな (する-verb + な prohibition: the rule wants an unconjugated verb, but the parser gives 混同する the ending する).
- **MeCab comparison not re-run:** inside this session's sandbox Python can't see %LOCALAPPDATA%\jlpt_devtools (bash can), so `mecab_compare.py` failed to import fugashi; run it from a normal terminal to measure the parser changes (last: CUT 63,600 / SWALLOW 26,155 sentences; its top CUTs were メアリー, フランス, ボストン — the names the katakana merge now joins).

**2026-10-15 (later): animated Amaterasu picture (not yet seen in Anki).** The owner animated Nihonjin → Amaterasu in Wallpaper Engine (projects in `H:/Main Program Files/Steam/steamapps/common/wallpaper_engine/projects/myprojects/`); only Amaterasu is wired in, the others are being remade. Her scene has play-once keyframes (all done by 5 s) over endless effects, so the OBS recording is cut into `media/amaterasu_intro.webm` (frames 0–449 of the 60 fps take, 7.5 s) and `media/amaterasu_loop.webm` (frames 450–1887 = 1,438 frames ≈ 5 shimmer cycles of ~288, picked by best end↔start match; its last 0.25 s crossfaded with frames 435–449 (was 1 s; owner: the fade itself was the visible part) so intro → loop and loop → loop don't jump); both 720×480, 30 fps, VP9, 0.26 + 0.75 MB. Recipe: `ffmpeg`, `setpts=N/60/TB` first, `[body][head]xfade=fade:duration=0.25:offset=T−0.25`, then every other frame by index (`select=not(mod(n,2)),setpts=N/30/TB`), `scale=720:480`, `libvpx-vp9 -crf 33`. Check the seam with `tblend=difference` + signalstats (the wrap should sit under the 90th percentile of normal motion), not psnr with a trimmed second input (its frame sync faked duplicates). `rank_video.py`: `RankVideo` (QMediaPlayer → QVideoSink → QImage frames; Anki's Qt ships the FFmpeg backend; the loop pre-rolls paused under the intro so the handoff is one frame; `ok` False → still picture). `RankBadge` plays it in the art label when the rank has a loop clip — **Amaterasu only in Amaterasu mode (20,000; owner)**, the still picture below it — pauses when hidden, keeps it across `update_info` for the same rank. **Ascension with the intro (owner):** the intro plays in the badge; `_ASCENSION["pending"]` makes a badge built during a real crossing start dark; `RankBadge.intro_light()` holds the divine light (DivineOverlay + badge sunburst) at 0 until her light blooms (`rank_video.INTRO_BLOOM` 0.12–0.55 s, measured) and fades it in with it; the flash (`_AscensionFlash`, now centred on the light behind her bun) starts at bloom − `RISE` (0.288 s) so it peaks with her light; the message comes when the intro ends (~7.5 s) instead of at 1.3 s. With the video the blown-out sky only melts the top edge (`AMATERASU_VIDEO_SKY_END` 0.14), so her animated rays and shimmer show. **Perf:** `DivineOverlay` now renders the whole view once per divine-clock tick and blits the needed part for every other repaint (cache key: phase, view, scale, video), since each video frame repainted the art spot and re-rendered the light (11 ms → 2 ms per paint offscreen; owner: Amaterasu mode felt a little laggy). Tested offscreen with PyQt6 6.11 (venv at `%TEMP%/qtv`, since Anki's PyQt is cp313-only and the system Python is 3.14): every intro frame arrives, timeline dark → bloom → flash → loop as designed. Package must include `media/`. **2026-10-16 owner test:** loop, fade and mode-only gating OK; fixed the first ☀ Force Amaterasu after opening not firing the ascension (`_rank_debug_prev_was_max` started as None and None → max wasn't a crossing; now set from the previewed state in `_build`). **Mini widget animated too (owner 2026-10-16):** in Amaterasu mode `_rank_html` uses a `<video autoplay loop muted>` of the loop (`mini_widget._loop_url` → `/_addons/<folder>/media/amaterasu_loop.webm`, exported by `setWebExports(__name__, r"media/.+.webm")` in `__init__`), the still art as its poster/fallback, same class so the rays and feathering apply (`video.jlpt-mini-art` aspect-ratio 3/2). Checked in Chromium: autoplays, 720×480, inside the divine badge. **Star ambience (owner 2026-10-16):** `ambience.py` + `media/star_*.ogg` (0.9 MB), made by `tools/star_ambience.js` from the Sun's p-mode spectrum sped up ~36,000× (ν_max 3.09 mHz → 110 Hz, Δν → 4.8 Hz, modes 91–120 Hz = the owner's "gamma 90–123 Hz" band; l=2 modes 0.32 Hz below l=0 beat slowly; stochastic mode envelopes; 2×/3× overtones for small speakers). Three layers, after the owner's "acoustic lifecycle of a newborn star": **Ignition** (8 s one-shot: flutter 3→40 pulses/s while 90→123 Hz, bell strike at `STRIKE` 0.55 s), **Ringing Sun** (60 s loop), **Protostar heartbeat** (4 s loop = one colossal beat, lub 55→33 Hz / dub 48→30 Hz at +0.75 s, rumble tail; 15 bpm). Ascension: ignition starts on the intro's first frame (`RankVideo.intro_shown`), so its strike lands with the bloom and the flash; Ringing Sun at the strike (0.8 s fade-in); first heartbeat `HEARTBEAT_PERIOD` (4 s) after the strike = the beat spacing (owner). Opening the window in Amaterasu mode: Ringing Sun fades in over 3 s, heartbeat 4 s later. `ProgressDialog._apply_gold` → `enter()`/`leave()` (skipped while `_ASCENSION["pending"]`), `done()` → `leave(700)`. QMediaPlayer + QAudioOutput at volume 0.5; Qt loops OGG gaplessly (checked with QAudioBufferOutput: continuous timestamps, exact sample count). The chime now only plays if the ambience can't. Silent offscreen test: ignition at intro 0.033 s, flash at 0.267 s, sun +0.55 s, heart +4.0 s after that, all stop on leave. **Heartbeat off for now (owner 2026-10-16):** `ambience.HEARTBEAT = False`; the file and timing stay, set True to bring it back. **Mute (owner yes, 2026-10-16):** Settings → Amaterasu group (only shown once unlocked) → "Play the star's sound" (`amaterasu_sound`, default true; `ambience.enabled()`); off = fully silent, no chime fallback; saving it off calls `leave()` at once.
