## 8. UI conventions & constants (keep consistent)

**Level colours** (in `jlpt_data`): N5 #4caf50/#1b5e20, N4 #8bc34a/#33691e, N3 #ff9800/#e65100, N2 #f44336/#b71c1c, N1 #9c27b0/#4a148c.

**Other colours:**
- Gold #f5c518 (accent #fff8c5); marked-found border #ffe066; suspended ✕ (legend/header) #e05a5a.
- Missing tiles: dark grey with the character tinted in the level colour.

**Formatting and layout:**
- Numbers: thousands separators everywhere; one decimal place (`format_pct`/`format_progress`); never round up to 100.0%.
- Tables: left-aligned. Big tables must be filled via `_fill_table` (it pauses repaints and auto-fit), because filling thousands of rows into a visible table with ResizeToContents columns likely froze/crashed Anki when switching the Missing Words sort.
- Pop-up windows and the main window get min/max buttons (`Qt.WindowType.WindowMinMaxButtonsHint`).

**Gold mode:**
- New bars: `LevelBar(..., force_max_gold=..., colors=..., clickable=...)`, then add them to `self._freq_bars` or `self._level_bars`.
- New labels: add them to `_set_level_label_gold`'s label list.

---

## 9. Developer workflow & gotchas

- **Not a git repo.** Make backups before risky edits (copy files to a scratch folder).
- **Environment:** Windows 11, local Python **3.14** at `C:\Users\benne\AppData\Local\Python\pythoncore-3.14-64\python.exe`. Anki is installed at `C:\Users\benne\AppData\Local\Programs\Anki` and bundles **Python 3.13 + PyQt6** (compiled `.pyc`, so it can't be imported from 3.14). **UI code cannot be rendered locally**, so verify logic headlessly and ask the owner to restart Anki and screenshot.
- **Syntax check:** `python -c "import ast;ast.parse(open('progress_dialog.py',encoding='utf-8').read())"`.
- **Headless test harness (`tools/harness.py`):**
  - Stubs `aqt`, reads a **copy** of the collection (`collection.anki2` is at `C:\Users\benne\AppData\Roaming\Anki2\Ben\`; copy it to `<scratch>/col_copy.anki2`, never touch the original) and runs the real `collect_words`/`analyse` with the live config from `meta.json`.
  - Run it as `python harness.py <scratch_dir>`; it prints the per-level counts, frequency tiers, etc.
  - `tools/anomaly_scan.py <scratch_dir>` runs a full detection audit: skipped notes, odd characters, suspicious matches, multi-claims, near-misses, homophones. Run it after any matching change.
  - Both expect `harness.py` to live in the same `<scratch_dir>` and `sqlite3`-readable `col_copy.anki2` there. Delete the copy afterwards.
- **Shell quirks:**
  - The PowerShell tool's safety parser sometimes misreads inline Python (`-replace`, `'/'`, `"\t".join`) as `Remove-Item` paths and blocks the whole command. Write Python to a file and run that, and keep cleanup commands separate.
  - Bash heredocs that go through Python string literals mangle `\n` and `\uXXXX` (a `\n` inside generated JS/Python strings became a real line break twice on 2026-10-08/09). Prefer the Edit tool for code edits. The Write/Edit tools themselves turn `\uXXXX` escapes into the literal characters, so for character ranges compare code points (`0x3400 <= ord(c) <= 0x9FFF`) instead of writing escapes.
  - Python is `C:\Users\benne\AppData\Local\Python\pythoncore-3.14-64\python.exe` (`python` on PATH is the Microsoft Store stub).
- **Mini widget HTML:** Anki's deck list sits inside `<center>`, which centres *every block* inside it (`-webkit-center`). `#jlpt-mini-widget` sets `text-align: left` to cancel it; anything meant to be centred must use `margin: auto` explicitly. Preview widget changes by rendering `_widget_html` to a file wrapped in `<center>`.
- **HTML pages for the owner to click through** (review lists with checkboxes): put them in the project folder (e.g. `tools/`) and open them in the Browser pane with a `file:///` URL. Files outside the project (the scratchpad) render as **static snapshots**, so checkboxes and buttons don't work there, and the same goes for SendUserFile previews. Delete the page once the owner has answered.
- **Local preview servers:** never use port **8765** (AnkiConnect; a stray preview server there broke the owner's card adding for hours). Use e.g. 47391 and stop the server right after the screenshot. UI-less HTML (mini widget) can be previewed by rendering `_widget_html` with a stub; Qt widgets can't.
- **Running add-on modules outside Anki:** stub `aqt` (`mw`, `gui_hooks` lists incl. `profile_did_open`, `state_did_change`, `reviewer_will_answer_card`, `reviewer_did_answer_card`; `aqt.qt` names; `aqt.utils.showWarning/tooltip`) and `anki.utils.ids2str`, as `tools/harness.py` does. With `python -I`, call `sys.stdout.reconfigure(encoding="utf-8")` or Japanese output crashes on cp1252.
- **Anki crash diagnosis:** `C:\Users\benne\AppData\Roaming\Anki2\crash.log` is usually empty. Ask the owner to run `anki-console.bat` from the Anki install folder to see tracebacks.
- After changing anything user-visible, update `README.md`. The "?" rank-info popup computes list counts dynamically.
- **English ↔ Amaterasu-mode text protocol (owner 2026-10-09). Every English UI text has a gold-mode Japanese version; keep them in step:**
  1. Add or change English UI text → add/adjust its Japanese in `divine_text.py` in the same change: `EXACT` (whole fixed text), `PATTERNS` (text with numbers / names, regex), `PHRASES` (pieces of built text), `HEADERS` (table columns), `PLACEHOLDERS` (search boxes). Natural Japanese; no English left beside it (no JA + EN duplicates in gold mode). **Exception: the owner's own English localisation of the Amaterasu message (`_AMATERASU_MESSAGE_EN`) always stays under the Japanese** — it's written, not a translation (round 18: it was removed by mistake and restored). Ask before removing any English the owner wrote.
  2. Run `python -I -X utf8 tools/check_gold_text.py` (works headless; needs no Anki). It scans `progress_dialog.py`, `kanji_view.py`, `history_view.py` for user-visible strings (QLabel / QPushButton / QCheckBox / setText / addItem / addTab / setTabText / setPlaceholderText / setWindowTitle / header labels), fills f-string placeholders with sample values, and lists any text that still has English words in gold mode. **It must print OK before the change is done.** Text that truly stays English (debug row, symbols, titles set directly in code) goes in its `STAYS_ENGLISH` with a reason; new sample values for placeholders in `SAMPLES`; proper names in `NAMES_OK`.
  3. If the text is set **after** the dialog is built (a toggle, a count, a status line, a refill), call `_regold(widget)` right after the `setText`, or it appears in English in gold mode (round 17). Widgets created in bulk (thousands of table buttons) should be created already gold/Japanese when `_GOLD_MODE["on"]`.
  4. Colours: no new colours in gold mode except gold, black (#0d0b08) and white in the white-gold light. Inline `color:` in labels is recoloured by the walker automatically (keeps ★ yellow / ✕ red / dark "missing" squares); painted text and new widget types need their own gold branch.
  - Not covered by the checker: tooltips and Settings (stay English by design) and the mini widget's HTML (`mini_widget.py`; check its gold branch by hand).

Pressing the loanword button (+1,778 frequency-only) would make Near Native 4,840.

- `tools/` has no dedicated script-rule check. To verify the rule, count JLPT known words whose list display contains kanji but whose matched card's display has none. The count must be 0 (it was 0 on 2026-10-08).

Totals are slightly below the list sizes because お+kanji duplicates are removed (and any words the owner suspends). Large unexplained swings in these numbers after a change mean something broke.

## 10b. Loanword-button review (DONE 2026-10-09)

- Pattern deduced from the owner's calls: *would an English speaker, hearing the word in context, land on the right thing?* Clipping is fine (デジカメ, リモコン), loose meanings are fine (クレーム, サービス, リンス, ロース, バスター, ボンド, ピアス), source language doesn't matter if English shares the word (ゼミ, コーヒー). Rejected: everyday meaning is a different thing (ストーブ, レンジ, パート, ハーフ, ヤンキー, スルー, ペイオフ) or not a loanword / not English-sounding (アルバイト, リュック, サボテン, ゴーヤー).
- Applied to all ~2,450 marked + 346 skipped katakana words. Final sets in jlpt_data.py: `COUNT_AS_ENGLISH` (499, overrides any JMdict tag: abbreviations + the 208 other-language words English shares), `FALSE_FRIENDS` (26), `NOT_ENGLISH` (12: native words in katakana キリ/コメ/クリ/イチジク/ハブ, sound words ドーン/シャー/ジャン, サボテン/ゴーヤー/チャンポン/アリラン). Button-eligible: 2,591 frequency + 486 JLPT entries.
- **ウ removed from the frequency list** (list-bullet artifact mapped to 鵜): `DROP_LEMMAS` in tools/build_freq.py; 詐称 moved up to #20,000. `freq_extra.txt` was NOT rebuilt (needs the LUW zip): its exact ranks past #13,155 are now 1 too high and 詐称 also appears there as #20,001 (harmless: top 20,000 is checked first). Rebuild it next time the LUW data is downloaded. **ア (#7,421) removed the same way on 2026-10-09** (owner request; あっ/あ kept); シャーマン moved up to #20,000.
- Owner had pressed the button (2,442 found); the 15 newly excluded words (+ ウ) were unmarked → 2,427 found (backup in %TEMP%/jlpt_backup_history/overrides_before_unmark_round2.json). The 208 newly English words need another button press.
- Dated-words button removed 2026-10-09 (see §2.4); its 15 suspensions cleared (backup overrides_before_unsuspend_dated.json). After that: N5 654/682, N4 602/640, N3 859/1,702, N2 426/1,824, N1 544/3,209; Near Native 5,101 (owner had pressed the loanword button).
- Pending owner checks in Anki: History tab + y-axis, hover meanings (7), Shift+click Jisho, frequency overrides.

## 11. Feature backlog (owner's verdicts, 2026-10-07)

**Status:** #9, #2, #7, #8 built and **tested in Anki by the owner (2026-10-07)**: suspended option, tile shading, Include rank, frequency/legend wording, rank-up banner (incl. Close-button fix and ✕), mini rank bar left-aligned, blurred Amaterasu art + reveal, widget-off toast fallback (appears bottom-left of the main window). Only the real review-time toast is unconfirmed (needs a natural belt crossing). 2026-10-08 work (list additions, ≈ ranks, Off-list tab, Near Native label) was checked headlessly; the owner restarts Anki to see it.

**#3 progress history: ✅ built 2026-10-08** (owner accepted all four recommendations below and added a **manual "Take snapshot now" button**). Verified headlessly: the rebuilt value for today equals the live counts exactly (rank 3,161, mature 2,682, every JLPT level and frequency tier; ignore-suspended on); 892 days from 2024-04-30; rebuild 0.16 s; +132 learned this week. Day one shows JLPT 403 because the ~378 marked-found words count from the first day. **Awaiting the owner's Anki restart + screenshot** (the Qt chart couldn't be rendered locally). Original proposal, for reference:
- **Track daily** (one snapshot per day): rank count (young + mature notes), mature cards, JLPT known per level + total, frequency coverage (Up to N5 … N1, Near Native).
- **Show** in a new **History** tab: line chart with 7 / 30 / 90 days / all-time selector and series toggles (rank / JLPT levels / frequency); "+N learned this week · +N this month". Optional "+N this week" under the rank on the Overview. **No projected dates** (owner).
- **Decision 1, backfill:** rebuild history from the past (recommended). Rank/mature from the revlog (graduation/maturity days); JLPT/frequency from note creation dates (nid). Limits: deleted notes, past found/suspended overrides, and it uses *today's* lists.
- **Decision 2, when to record:** automatically once per day on profile open / deck-list render (recommended) vs only when the add-on opens.
- **Decision 3, storage:** `user_files/` (survives add-on updates). Also move `overrides.json` there automatically, keeping a backup (recommended), since Anki deletes the add-on folder on update.
- **Decision 4, list changes:** recalculate the backfilled history with the current lists (recommended) vs keep recorded points (shows jumps on list-change days).
- **Charting:** custom QPainter chart in the bars' glowing style with hover values; no new dependency.

**#4 meanings on hover: ✅ built 2026-10-08** (owner: only JLPT + frequency-list words; no meaning for other Off-list cards; first 7 meanings (raised from 3 at the owner's request); plus Shift+click → Jisho on any tile). Checked headlessly; awaiting the owner's restart + screenshot.

Next: #5 phases 2 and 3 (below). #1 became click-to-copy (2026-10-09).

**#5 Kanji & Grammar (owner spec 2026-10-09; phase 1 built, phases 2–3 to do):**
- **Sources (Settings).** Each of vocab / kanji / grammar can come from a **discrete field** OR the **auto parser**, in any combination:
  - Vocab: deck + note type → word field (+ reading field) [exists]; or parse a **Sentence field** (new column in the note-type mappings, phase 3).
  - Kanji: from the vocab fields when they're set (default, `kanji_source.mode = "vocab"`), a discrete field in another deck (`"fields"`, built), or sentences (phase 3: `"sentence"`, every kanji in the sentence fields; no parser needed).
  - Grammar: a **separate grammar deck + note type → grammar field** (phase 2), or parsed from sentences (phase 3).
  - Owner's own setup: vocab discrete (Kaishi Word/WordReading, Lapis Expression/ExpressionReading), kanji auto from the vocab fields, grammar discrete. Use case for the parser: people who mine plain sentence cards with no target word, no kanji deck and no grammar deck.
- **Sentence cards:** when vocab comes only from sentences (no word/reading fields), **every recognised word in a learned sentence card counts** as known (that card's maturity/score). A sentence card is still one note for the rank.
- **Parser (phase 3):** pure Python, bundled, no install (Anki's Python can't load MeCab). Yomitan-style longest dictionary match + deinflection rules (食べなかった → 食べる) over our JLPT + frequency words and their alternate spellings. Grammar: OpenJLPT patterns with conjugation-aware rules. **Owner: detect the right ones, don't inflate**: only distinctive patterns are auto-detected; short/ambiguous ones (〜て, 〜の, 〜が) are manual only (middle-click found).
- **Grammar UI (phase 2):** All Grammar bar + compact N5–N1 bars on the same tab; their grids open only from the bars. Grammar known = learned card in the grammar deck whose field matches a pattern, or marked found. OpenJLPT grammar JSON fields: id, pattern (〜がてら), romaji, level, meaning, formation, examples[ja/furigana/en], tags, notes. 526 points: N5 81, N4 98, N3 101, N2 123, N1 123.
- **Phase 2 status (2026-10-09):** the owner has **no grammar deck yet** (plans one). None is in the "Ben" profile either (decks: ベンの辞書, Kaishi Radicals, JLPT N5/N4/N3 Kanji Decks last reviewed Feb–Mar 2025, archives). Ask where it is and what its pattern field looks like before building the matcher. (He also has three passjapanesetest.com kanji decks, field `kanji`; he chose kanji from vocab.)

**#14 parked plan (Susanoo + Tsukuyomi).** Owner said a global rank change is fine (no users yet), so the preferred shape is a **full rebuild**: 12 ranks × 8 belts = 96 belts, each ~4% bigger than the last (r = 1.04, first belt ≈ 19 cards, last ≈ 787), scaled to 20,000; Amaterasu reshaped as now (White–Brown = first half, ~394 cards per belt; Black = 10 degrees of ~306 cards). Needs: two pictures in the existing style (placeholder: kanji on the rank colours until then), colours (suggested: Susanoo storm, deep indigo + lightning blue; Tsukuyomi moonlight, midnight blue + silver-white), and every 10-rank assumption updated ("?" window, rank cards, toasts, mini widget, share image, gold-mode text, history snapshots, README). Times below assume N newly learned words a day:

| Rank | Cards | In rank @10/day | In rank @20/day | Reached by @10/day | Reached by @20/day |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| Gaijin | 0 – 175 | 18 days | 9 days | 18 days | 9 days |
| Tourist | 175 – 414 | 24 days | 12 days | 41 days | 3 weeks |
| Weeb | 414 – 741 | 1 month | 16 days | 2.4 months | 5 weeks |
| Exchange Student | 741 – 1,189 | 1.5 months | 3 weeks | 3.9 months | 2 months |
| Naijin | 1,189 – 1,803 | 2 months | 1 month | 6 months | 3 months |
| Nihonjin | 1,803 – 2,642 | 2.8 months | 1.4 months | 8.7 months | 4.3 months |
| Samurai | 2,642 – 3,790 | 3.8 months | 2 months | 1 year | 6 months |
| Shogun | 3,790 – 5,362 | 5.2 months | 2.6 months | 1.5 years | 9 months |
| Emperor | 5,362 – 7,513 | 7 months | 3.5 months | 2 years | 1 year |
| Susanoo | 7,513 – 10,457 | 10 months | 5 months | 2.9 years | 1.4 years |
| Tsukuyomi | 10,457 – 14,486 | 13 months | 6.6 months | 4 years | 2 years |
| Amaterasu | 14,486 – 20,000 | 18 months | 9 months | 5.5 years | 2.7 years |

Alternatives considered: (1) keep ranks through Samurai, top five nearly equal (~2,600–3,100 each; Amaterasu shrinks to 3,067); (middle) keep through Samurai, top five each ~12% longer (Shogun 5,916 → Emperor 8,133 → Susanoo 10,616 → Tsukuyomi 13,397 → Amaterasu 16,512 → 20,000). Both were only for protecting existing ranks, which no longer matters.

**Details agreed (2026-10-07):**
- **#2 saturation:** first built with learning as its own subtle level; the owner then asked to **merge new + learning** into one colour and label. Now 3 visible levels: new/learning, young, mature (full). A note with several cards uses its **most mature** card.
- **#7 rank:** relearning (lapsed) cards count as **young**. Rank is recomputed on every scan, so it can go down; the toast only fires on an upward crossing.
- **Build order (chosen for fewest conflicts):** #9 → #2 → #7 + #8 → #3 → #4 → #1 → #5.

## 11b. Release & monetization plan (owner, 2026-10-15) — NOT BUILT, after release-ready

**2026-10-20 (owner): monetization reshaped.** (1) **Loyalty badge = time with Kotodama, no money involved** — BUILT (§0 entry of 2026-10-20). (2) **"I support Kotodama" / "Supported Kotodama" line** on the shareable parts (share image + Nakama card), with real verification of a donation — NOT BUILT. Plan: Ko-fi webhook → the Nakama Cloudflare Worker (verify Ko-fi's verification token), the add-on's Nakama tab shows a claim code the donor puts in the Ko-fi message, the server sets `supporter: since <date>` on that profile and keeps no email / amount; refunds remove it; disclose in the privacy line. Only the Nakama card is truly verified (built from server data); the share image is drawn locally, so there it's honour-level. Recommendation given: **don't lock the share image behind Nakama** (it's free advertising; offline users keep it); show the supporter line only when Nakama confirms it — owner said gating "might" be needed, not decided. One plain line, the same for every amount, no visual perks. (3) **Licence:** owner asked whether NINJAL must be asked; answer: not strictly for a thank-you line that unlocks nothing (small risk; donor credits are normal for non-commercial projects); the loyalty badge doesn't touch it at all; emailing NINJAL stays a good idea for the ND issue (our trimmed frequency files) and any future paid change — optional, drafting offered. Earlier ideas (paid cosmetics, Discord-style paid badge tiers) are dropped.

**2026-10-16 (owner): launch with only the Ko-fi one-time donation button** (the "♥ Support" placement below); ribbons and supporter cosmetics are not part of the initial release.

Order: release-ready first (parser vocab accuracy → measured like grammar), then the roadmap doc (features × nearest competitor × our angle; licence audit of bundled data: JMdict CC BY-SA, Tatoeba CC BY, OpenJLPT, BCCWJ list, kanji data — check commercial-use terms before any paid perk; check AnkiWeb rules on donation links / paid features), then these:
- **Support button:** always visible in the main window, not per tab: bottom-left of the dialog's bottom row (Close is bottom-right, the row stays across tab switches). Quiet style ("♥ Support"), never a popup.
- **Rank-up ribbons on the share image:** each rank-up opens a 24-hour window (owner: nobody needs longer to press Share; the saved PNG is theirs forever; the share dialog shows the time left, e.g. "Rank-up ribbon: 14 h left"; the ribbon's date is the rank-up date, not the share date) during which the share dialog offers a ribbon ("Rank up · 侍 Samurai · date"); an option in the share menu, off = the normal card. Chasing the next ribbon is the reason to come back. Amaterasu (top rank, nothing after it) gets the same ribbon style with only its unlock date, no window, available forever. Ranks can drop (lapses), so record the FIRST date each rank was reached: dropping and re-rising doesn't reopen a window. Belt-ups get no ribbon (owner), so rank ribbons stay special.
- **Supporter cosmetics (recurring donations), honour-system unlock, no server:** must never look like anything earned. Earned = rank picture, glow, belt colours (unchanged by money). Paid = frame around the badge (ink-brush ring, hanko seal, kamon crest), a background scene behind it, name-plate style, share-card paper/theme. One theme choice applies to both the share image and the Overview (fewer settings).
- **Positioning:** grammar coverage of your own deck/sentences and of any text is the strongest unique claim (measured 97% precision / 85% recall on fresh Tatoeba samples, round 4); word coverage needs the same measured accuracy before it can be advertised.

### 2026-10-27 (second session): loanwords join gradually, marks reach only their own word

**This session (owner checked everything in Anki 2026-10-27, all good; the kana-card change too):**
- **Marked English loanwords join every count gradually**, not just the rank (§2.4): `card_state.hold_back_loans` runs after each analysis (window + mini widget) and moves loanwords the rank doesn't count yet out of the JLPT known words and the frequency tiers; `results["loans_waiting"]` keeps them. `found_parts` is cached in results (always the full marked list). Word Grid: waiting ones sit in list order among the missing, drawn as missing with a faint ★ (`WordCell(waiting=…)`, `#7a6a3a`); the Missing tab leaves them out. History dates each loanword to the day the cards reached it. Cause: WIGGLE's Nakama card showed 344 words beside 850 JLPT words (2,586 marked, 4 counted).
- **A word is an English loanword if either list's test says so** (`card_state.is_loan`), everywhere marks are split into "loanwords" / "marked found".
- **Marks reach only their own word** (§5b): katakana marks only reach katakana frequency rows (the loanword button had also marked がん via ガン, 鯛 via タイ …: the owner's "6 words marked found"); `word_rank` tiers (own spelling first; kana never lands on a kanji row that only lists it as a side form). 91 JLPT words moved to their right row (ほうき was 放棄, 街 was 町).
- **Nakama ⟳ Refresh recounts first** (the window's full refresh), so your card carries your latest marks. Nakama card / share image: "N English loanwords", "N words marked found" (`share_card._loans_line`, one line for both).
- **Kanji & Grammar:** a Refresh left the old intro + ⟳ drawn over the page; `update_results` now deletes every child but the gold overlay. Other tabs checked: fine.
- **Progress Review:** "+N outside your goal" is a link only (no word list). Days with 0 words draw no bar.
- **Loanword "?" pop-up** rewritten: gradual everywhere, one order (rank counts all counted ones; frequency bars those in the top 20,000; JLPT bars those on a list), JLPT-only loanwords take their level's place (N5 800 … N1 13,800).
